> **AI-powered trading firm** za Bolha, Facebook Marketplace, Vinted, Avtonet, mobile.de, Kleinanzeigen, Subito, Willhaben in Quoka.
> **432 AI endpointov** + **84 analytics** + **18 cron automatizacij** + **11 Telegram ukazov** + **11 platform** za iskanje, ocenjevanje, kupovanje in preprodajo.
>
> ## 🎯 Feature Overview (v8.63-90)
>
> ### Intelligence Suite (v8.63-70)
> - **🏷️ Trade Tags** (v8.63) — fleksibilna semantična kategorizacija z per-tag performance analitiko
> - **🔥 Sell Priority** (v8.65) — 0-100 urgency score za held trades + Top 3 Action Panel
> - **💡 Smart Pricing** (v8.66) — data-driven suggested sell price z confidence + comparables
> - **🏆 Outcome Scorecard** (v8.67) — post-sale quality analysis (pricing/timing/outcome score)
> - **🛒 Buy Opportunity** (v8.68-69) — buy score za listings + persist v Trade ob nakupu
> - **🧠 Decision Accuracy** (v8.70) — meta-analiza: ali algoritmi delujejo? (correlation + grade A-F)
>

> - **🏆 Compare + AI Advisor** (v8.72) — side-by-side comparison + Winner z Absolute Recommendation
> - **🌍 Cross-Platform Prices** (v8.74) — "iPhone na Bolha avg 450€, na Quoka avg 380€"
>

> ## 🏗️ v9.x — Architecture & Quality (v8.94–v9.23)

> ### Modularizacija (v8.94–v9.09)

> - Inkrementalna ekstrakcija: 7 podkomponent iz settings (v8.95-v8.96), 25 iz ai-hub (v8.97-v8.98), 25 iz trades (v8.99, v9.07-v9.08), 6 iz listings (v9.00), 26 iz statistics (v9.01-v9.02), 6 iz monitors (v9.03), 7 iz dashboard (v9.04), 7 iz analytics (v9.05), 4 iz iskalnik (v9.06), 3 iz watchlist (v9.09), 11 iz inventory (v9.09), 11 iz buyers (v9.09), 11 iz pricing (v9.09), 11 iz risk (v9.09), 4 iz alerts (v9.09), 11 iz listing-optimization (v9.09)

### Performance Optimization (v9.23)
> - **React.memo** na 4 ključne list komponente: TradeRow, ResultCard, WatchlistItemCard, StatCard
> - Preprečuje nepotrebne re-render-e pri parent state changes
> - Dashboard auto-refresh (30s): re-render samo spremenjenih card-ov
> - Trades view (25 items): re-render samo spremenjene trgovine
>

> **v8.72.0 — 🏆 Result Comparison View + AI Buy Advisor (Polish phase continues — "Kateri od 5 VW Golf 5 naj kupim? Side-by-side primerjava z AI pick"):** Problem: Iskalnik (v8.71) mi da 5 VW Golf 5 rezultatov (9500€ - 19500€) ampak ne vem KATEREGA kupiti. Najcenejši (9500€) ima 125000 km in tretji lastnik. Najdražji (19500€ GTI) je premium ampak morda preveč. Potrebujem side-by-side primerjavo z AI priporočilom. v8.72 dodaja: (1) **Compare API** `/api/search/compare` (NEW, POST `{listingIds}`) — multi-listing comparison. Compute buy scores per listing, determine winner (highest buyScore, tiebreaker price), cheapest, bestAI. Advisor insights: winner highlight, cheapest delta from winner, best AI score, discount%, expected ROI, risk warnings (≥6/10), price drop notes. (2) **IskalnikView multi-select** — checkbox na vsakem rezultatu (max 6), selection bar z count + Počisti + Primerjaj button. (3) **CompareDialog** — side-by-side comparison table z 13 vrsticami (Cena, Buy Score, AI Score/Risk/Verdict, AI Estimated Value, Discount%, Expected ROI, Letnik, Lokacija, Prodajalec, Vir, Padec cene) z winner column highlighted emerald. AI Buy Advisor insights section z 6+ actionable priporočili. Winner highlight card z recommendation. Summary stats (count, min/max price, avg buy score). LIVE: VW Golf 5 primerjava 3 listingov → Winner: VW Golf 5 2.0 TDI 2020 Novo Mesto (55/100 BUY, 13900€, AI 8/10, Risk 2/10, -6% pod oceno, +6% ROI). Najcenejši: VW Golf 5 1.4 TSI 2018 Celje (9500€, 4400€ razlike od winnerja). Stats: AI endpointi 432 (nespremenjeto), Total API routes 641→642 (+1: search/compare), funkcije 243→244 (+1: CompareDialog). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Browser verification: dialog open, AI advisor insights (3 priporočila), winner highlight, 13-row comparison table, 0 console errors. **Naravni naslednji korak po Iskalniku: 'najdi 5 rezultatov' → 'primerjaj' → 'AI pove kupi ta'.**
>
> **v8.71.0 — 🔍 Iskalnik: Targeted Item Search + Proxy Buying (Polish phase continues — "Iščem VW Golf 5, 2020, Ljubljana → najdi najcenejši + cel opis + kraj"):** Problem: Uporabnik želi iskati po vseh oglasih z specifičnimi kriteriji (artikel, cena, kraj, letnik) in dobiti rezultate sortirane po najcenejši/best score/najbližji — ne more ker listings-view ima samo filter po monitorju. Prav tako želi možnost 'iščem za nekoga' (proxy buying — iščem za mamo, kolega, itd.). v8.71 dodaja: (1) **BuyRequest model** (NEW) — saved search z searchFor (oseba za kero iščemo), title, keywords, category, priceMin/Max, location, yearMin/Max, condition, sortBy, notes. Indexi na [isActive], [category], [searchFor]. (2) **Search API** `/api/search/items` (NEW) — GET z q/category/priceMin/priceMax/location/yearMin/yearMax/verdict/sortBy parametri. Year filter parsan iz title/description (regex \b(19|20)\d{2}\b). Sort: cheapest (price asc), best_score (computeBuyScore per listing), newest (firstSeenAt desc), closest (location text match), price_drop (priceDroppedAt desc). Vrne results z fullDescription (detailDescription ali description), buyScore, aiScore/risk/verdict/estimatedValue, sellerName, monitor info. (3) **Buy Requests CRUD API** `/api/buy-requests` (GET list + POST create) + `/api/buy-requests/[id]` (PUT update + DELETE). (4) **Iskalnik View** (NEW — `src/components/dashboard/iskalnik-view.tsx`) — forma z iskalnim nizom + 'Iščem za' field (proxy buying) + 8 filtrov (kategorija, cena min/max, lokacija, letnik min/max, AI verdict, sort). Rezultati: rank badge (#1 emerald, #2 primary, #3 amber), naslov, cena (emerald bold), AI ocena vrednosti (line-through če drugačna), padec cene badge, lokacija z MapPin, datum, AI score/risk, buy score badge (🛒), AI verdict badge. Expandable cel opis oglasa (detailDescription) + AI reason + seller info. 'Prikaži cel oglas' toggle. Saved searches panel — klik za load kriterijev, × za delete. Save dialog z searchFor + notes. (5) **Navigation** — 'Iskalnik' dodan med Oglasi in Watchlist z Search ikono. (6) **Seed scripts** — `scripts/seed-listings.ts` (8 elektronika) + `scripts/seed-cars.ts` (7 avto: VW Golf 5 razni letniki/lokacije, BMW 320d, Audi A4). LIVE: iskanje 'VW Golf 5' → 5 rezultatov sortiranih najcenejši: 1) 9500€ Celje (2018), 2) 12800€ Maribor (2019), 3) 13900€ Novo Mesto (2020), 4) 14500€ Ljubljana (2020), 5) 19500€ Kranj (2021 GTI). 'Najdenih 5 oglasov · najcenejši: 9500€'. Stats: AI endpointi 432 (nespremenjeto), Total API routes 638→641 (+3: search/items, buy-requests, buy-requests/[id]), funkcije 242→243 (+1: IskalnikView). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Browser verification: Iskalnik view se naloži, iskanje 'VW Golf 5' → 5 rezultatov, najcenejši 9500€, 0 console errors. **Nova funkcija: 'Iščem VW Golf 5, 2020, max 15000€, Ljubljana' → najdi najcenejši + cel opis + kraj + buy score. Plus 'iščem za nekoga' za proxy buying.**

> **v8.70.0 — 🧠 Decision Accuracy Analytics (Polish phase continues — "Ali moji algoritmi dejansko delujejo? Meta-analiza intelligence suite"):** Problem: v8.65-69 sem zgradil celotno intelligence suite (buy score, sell priority, smart price, outcome). Ampak ne vem ali MOJI ALGORITMI DELAJOJO! Ali visok buy score dejansko korelira z visokim outcome-om? Ali smart price predlogi so v resnici blizu dejanskih prodajnih cen? Brez te meta-analize ne vem ali mi algoritmi pomagajo ali me zavajajo. v8.70 dodaja: (1) **Buy Score vs Outcome correlation** — Pearson correlation coefficient med buyScore in outcomeScore. Accuracy % = delež tradov kjer buy score ≥50 napoveduje outcome ≥50 (ali <50 napoveduje <50). 4 bucket-i (0-25, 26-50, 51-75, 76-100) z avgOutcome/profit/winRate + verdict (EXCELLENT/GOOD/POOR/INVERTED). highScoreAvgOutcome vs lowScoreAvgOutcome za diferenciacijo. (2) **Smart Price accuracy** — % prodaj znotraj [suggestedMin, suggestedMax], % nad max (overpriced/lucky), % pod min (underpriced), avg deviation %. (3) **Overall Intelligence Health** — score 0-100 z grade A-F + insights (Slovenian actionable). Formula: base 50 + buy score accuracy bonus + correlation bonus + smart price accuracy bonus. (4) **API** — `/api/analytics/decision-accuracy` GET. (5) **DecisionAccuracyCard** — dashboard card z: Intelligence Health grade badge (A-F z color-coding emerald/primary/amber/orange/red), Buy Score Accuracy (correlation, accuracy %, buckets mini-bar vizualizacija z color-coded verdict-i), Smart Price Accuracy (3-column: v obsegu/nad max/pod min), insights list z warning/good icons. LIVE: 19 sold trades — Smart Price 84% within range, 11% avg deviation (DOBRO kalibriran ✓). Buy Score 0 prodaj z persisted score (v8.69 shranjuje samo za nove kupčije). Overall Grade C (55/100) — zmerna kalibracija, potrebno več buy-score podatkov. Top insight: 'Smart price predlogi so v 84% primerov znotraj obsega — dobro kalibriran.' Stats: AI endpointi 432 (nespremenjeto), Total API routes 637→638 (+1: analytics/decision-accuracy), funkcije 241→242 (+1: DecisionAccuracyCard). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Browser verification: DecisionAccuracyCard visible na dashboard, Grade C badge, 84% smart price accuracy, insights visible, 0 console errors. **CAPSTONE: zapre learning loop z validacijo — 'ali moji algoritmi dejansko delujejo?'. Ko zbereš več buy-score podatkov, boš videl ali buy score korelira z outcome-om (STRONG correlation = algoritem deluje, INVERTED = popravi uteži).**
>
> **v8.69.0 — 🛒 Buy Score Badge v ListingCard + Buy→Trade Linkage (Polish phase continues — "Persist buy intelligence ob nakupu za feedback loop učenja"):** Problem: v8.68 mi pove KATERI listings so STRONG_BUY ampak te podatke vidim samo na dashboard card-u. V sami Listings view teh badge-ov NI — moram si zapomniti 'Pioneer je 100' in ga potem poiskati med 100+ oglasi. Prav tako: ko kliknem 'Kupi' na listing, se pretvori v Trade ampak izgubim buy score kontekst — kasneje v Outcome (v8.67) ne vem kako dober nakup je bil v času odločitve. v8.69 dodaja: (1) **Schema** — `buyScore Int?`, `buyVerdict String?`, `buyScoreAt DateTime?` na Trade model. Persistira buy intelligence ob nakupu za kasnejšo primerjavo z outcome (v8.67). (2) **`/api/trades` POST (fromListingId mode)** — avtomatsko compute-a buy score ob nakupu preko `getBuyOpportunityForListing()` in shrani v Trade. Non-critical — če compute fail-a, buy proceed-a brez score-a. (3) **ListingRow buy score badge** — `🛒 score` z verdict-based color-coding: emerald=STRONG_BUY (≥75), primary=BUY (≥55), amber=CONSIDER (≥35), red=AVOID (<35). Hover tooltip: pričakovan ROI, pričakovan dobiček, discount% pod AI oceno, recommendation. (4) **ListingsView batch fetch** — `buyScoreMap` state, fetch-a `/api/analytics/buy-opportunity?limit=50` ko se listings spremenijo. Auto-refresh. (5) **TradeRow original buy score badge** — prikaže `🛒 score` za trades z persisted buy context. Tooltip poveže buy score z outcome score (v8.67): 'outcome 92/100 — PERFECT' ali 'še ni prodano'. Omogoča feedback loop: 'buy score 85 → outcome 92 = odlična odločitev' ali 'buy score 38 → outcome 25 = slab nakup, izboljšaj buy strategijo'. LIVE: 8 listings prikazanih z buy score badge-i v Oglasi view (🛒 77 za iPhone 13 Pro, itd.). 0 console errors. Stats: AI endpointi 432 (nespremenjeto), Total API routes 637 (nespremenjeto — schema/UI only), funkcije 240→241 (+1: Buy→Trade linkage). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Browser verification: 🛒 badges prikazani v ListingRow, 'Prikazano 8 od 8 oglasov', 0 console errors. **KOMPLETIRA BUY/SELL CYCLE z UČENJEM: v8.68 (KUPITI score) + v8.69 (persist + prikaz v ListingCard + TradeRow) + v8.67 (outcome comparison) = feedback loop 'sem dobro ocenil nakup?'. Popolna trade intelligence z buy + sell + feedback loop za izboljšanje strategije.**
>
> **v8.68.0 — 🛒 Buy Opportunity Score za Listings (Polish phase continues — "Ali naj kupim ta oglas? Data-driven buy intelligence"):** Problem: Sell decision loop je zaključen (v8.65-67 — KDAJ prodati, ZA KOLIKO, ali je bilo prav). Ampak BUY side ni pokrit! Ko vidim oglas 'iPhone 13 Pro 256GB - 450€' na Bolha, ne vem ali naj ga kupim ali ne. Imam AI verdicts (PRILIKA/SUMNJIVO) ampak ne data-driven buy score-a ki upošteva mojo zgodovino trgovin. v8.68 dodaja: (1) **Compute module** — `src/lib/trades/buy-opportunity.ts` (NEW) — `computeBuyScore(listing, context)` vrača `BuyOpportunityResult` z: `score` (0-100), `verdict` (STRONG_BUY/BUY/CONSIDER/AVOID), `expectedROI` (%), `expectedProfit` (EUR), `suggestedMaxBuyPrice` (max price for 15% target ROI), `discountPercent` (% below AI estimated value), `confidence` (0-100), `reasoning[]`, `recommendation` (slovensko). Heuristic formula: base 25 + price vs AI estimatedValue (discount% × 1.5, max +30) + price vs marketAvgSellPrice (margin% × 0.8, max +25) + aiScore × 2.5 (max +25) - aiRisk × 2 (max -20) + category STAR (+10) / UNDERPERFORMER (-10) + price drop (+5). Verdict: STRONG_BUY ≥75, BUY ≥55, CONSIDER ≥35, AVOID <35. (2) **Context fetch** — `getBuyOpportunityForListing(listingId)` fetch-a sold trades in same category (derived from monitor.tags) za marketAvgSellPrice + marketAvgROI + categoryVerdict. `getTopBuyOpportunities(limit)` batch. (3) **API** — `/api/analytics/buy-opportunity` GET (?listingId=xxx za single, ?limit=N za batch top). (4) **BuyOpportunitySummaryCard** — `src/components/dashboard/buy-opportunity-summary-card.tsx` (NEW) — dashboard card z: verdict distribution bar (STRONG_BUY/BUY/CONSIDER/AVOID z counts + bar vizualizacija), Top 3 priložnosti z score badge + price + expectedROI + discount%, stats footer (X za kupnino, Y premislek, Z izogib). Uses useFetch + CardSkeleton + CardError. (5) **Seed script** — `scripts/seed-listings.ts` — 8 demo listings z AI evalvacijo (iPhone 13 Pro 450€, Samsung S22 380€, MacBook Air M1 720€, Sony A7III 1100€, Pioneer radio 90€, PS5 550€, Nike Jordan 75€, Bosch vijačni 28€). LIVE: 8 listings — 5 STRONG_BUY (Avto radio Pioneer 100, Bosch vijačni 94, Nike Air Jordan 93, iPhone 13 Pro 77, Samsung S22 76), 2 CONSIDER, 1 AVOID. Top 3: Pioneer radio (90€, +360% ROI, -25% pod oceno), Bosch vijačni (28€, +1379% ROI, -20% pod oceno), Nike Jordan (75€, +452% ROI, -21% pod oceno). Stats: AI endpointi 432 (nespremenjeto), Total API routes 636→637 (+1: analytics/buy-opportunity), funkcije 239→240 (+1: BuyOpportunitySummaryCard). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Browser verification: BuyOpportunitySummaryCard visible na dashboard z Top 3 priložnosti, verdict distribution bar (5/2/1), 0 console errors. **KOMPLETIRA BUY/SELL CYCLE: v8.68 (KUPITI?) + v8.65 (KDAJ prodati) + v8.66 (ZA KOLIKO) + v8.67 (ali je bilo prav?) = popolna trade intelligence z buy + sell + feedback loop.**
>
> **v8.67.0 — 🏆 Trade Outcome Scorecard (Polish phase continues — "Ali sem prodal optimalno? Koliko denarja sem pustil na mizi?"):** Problem: v8.65 (priority) + v8.66 (smart price) dajata popolno PRE-SALE inteligenco. Ampak po prodaji ne vem ali sem prodal po optimalni ceni, ali je bil timing pravi, koliko denarja sem pustil na mizi. LIVE: 19 sold trades, nimam feedback loop-a za izboljšanje pricing strategije. v8.67 dodaja: (1) **Compute module** — `src/lib/trades/outcome-score.ts` (NEW) — `computeOutcomeScore(trade, context)` vrača `OutcomeResult` z: `pricingScore` (0-100, actual sellPrice vs smart price range iz v8.66), `timingScore` (0-100, hold days vs category avg 0.5-1.5x optimal), `outcomeScore` (0-100, profit/ROI quality), `overallScore` (weighted 40% pricing + 25% timing + 35% outcome), `verdict` (PERFECT/GOOD/ACCEPTABLE/SUBOPTIMAL/LOSS), `leftOnTable` (EUR — positive = underpriced, negative = overpriced/lucky), `reasoning[]`, `lessons[]` (Slovenian actionable). Pure compute, uses computeSmartPrice from v8.66. (2) **Aggregate summary** — `getOutcomeSummary()` vrača `OutcomeSummary` z: perfect/good/acceptable/suboptimal/loss counts, avgScores, totalLeftOnTable, totalExtraGained, avgLeftOnTable, best/worst outcome, topLessons (most common patterns). (3) **API** — `/api/analytics/outcome-score` GET (?tradeId=xxx za single, brez za batch summary). (4) **TradeRow outcome badge** — za sold trades prikaže 🏆/✓/○/✗/△ + overallScore. leftOnTable indicator (-XX€ za underpriced). Hover tooltip: verdict, 3 sub-scores, left on table, lekcije. Color-coded: emerald=PERFECT, primary=GOOD, amber=ACCEPTABLE, red=LOSS. (5) **Sort opcija 'Outcome ↓'** — nov option v sort dropdown, sorts by overallScore desc. > **v8.66.0 — 💡 Smart Pricing Recommender (Polish phase continues — "Za koliko naj prodam held trade?"):** Problem: v8.65 mi pove KDAJ prodati (priority score). Ampak ko kliknem 'Prodaj' na Sony A7III (kupljeno 800€), ne vem za koliko naj jo prodam. Quick Sell form predlaga `buyPrice × 1.3` (formula) ampak ne upošteva zgodovinskih prodajnih cen, ciljne ROI, dni v skladišču, tag verdict. v8.66 dodaja: (1) **Compute module** — `src/lib/trades/smart-pricing.ts` (NEW) — `computeSmartPrice(trade, context)` vrača `SmartPriceResult` z: `suggestedMin` (cost×1.05), `suggestedMax` (cost×2.0), `suggestedOptimal` (sweet spot), `expectedProfit`, `expectedROI` (%), `confidence` (0-100), `confidenceLabel` (HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW), `reasoning[]`, `comparables[]`. Heuristic formula: base = cost × (1 + targetROI/100) kjer targetROI = max(15%, categoryAvgROI - 5%) (slightly below avg za hitro prodajo). Blend 60% base + 40% comparable avg sell price. Adjustments: days held >30d → -3%/10d (max -15%); STAR tag → +5% premium; UNDERPERFORMER tag → -10%; goal progress <50% → -5%. Clamp [cost×1.05, cost×2.0]. Confidence: 30 base + comparables (5+=40, 3+=30, 1+=15) + categoryAvgROI (+20) + tags (+10). (2) **Context fetch** — `getSmartPriceForTrade(tradeId)` fetch-a comparables (same category, buyPrice ±50%) + category avg ROI + monthly goal progress iz sold trades. `getSmartPricesForAllHeld()` batch. (3) **API** — `/api/analytics/smart-pricing` GET (?tradeId=xxx za single, brez za batch). (4) **TradeRow price badge** — 💡 + suggestedOptimal + confidence indicator (★ HIGH / ● MEDIUM / ○ LOW). Hover tooltip: obseg, pričakovan ROI, zaupanje, comparables count, razlogi. Color: primary tint. (5) **Held Action Panel** — 'Predlagana cena' row na Top 3 karticah z suggestedOptimal + tooltip z obseg/ROI/zaupanje. (6) **Quick Sell pre-fill** — Quick Sell form sedaj pre-fill-a z `priceHint.suggestedOptimal` namesto `buyPrice × 1.3` (fallback če ni hint-a). LIVE: 5 held trades — Sony A7III camera (856€ optimal, 5% ROI, 1 comp, HIGH ★), Xbox Series X (426€, 22% ROI, 4 comps, HIGH ★), PlayStation 5 (475€, 19% ROI, 4 comps, HIGH ★), Nike Air Max 97 (119€, 25% ROI, 3 comps, HIGH ★), Stanley ketan set (50€, 85% ROI, 1 comp, HIGH ★). Stats: AI endpointi 432 (nespremenjeto), Total API routes 634→635 (+1: analytics/smart-pricing), funkcije 237→238 (+1: Smart Pricing Recommender). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Browser verification: 💡 badges prikazani ('💡 855.75€★'), 'Predlagana cena' panel visible, 0 console errors. **Polish phase continues — v8.66 (Smart Pricing Recommender). "Za koliko naj prodam?" — odgovorjen z data-driven suggested price + confidence + comparables.**

> **v8.65.0 — 🔥 Sell Priority Score za Held Trades (Polish phase continues — "Kateri held trade naj najprej prodaj?"):** Problem: Vsak dan odprem Skladišče → 5 held trade-ov → ne vem kateri naj hitro prodam. Trade Insights (v8.40) imajo optimal hold days ampak ne per-trade prioritizacijo. v8.65 dodaja: (1) **Compute module** — `src/lib/trades/sell-priority.ts` (NEW) — `computeSellPriority(trade, context)` vrača `SellPriorityResult` z: `score` (0-100), `level` (HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW), `reasons[]` (kategorija + impact), `recommendedAction` (slovensko). Heuristic formula: base 30 + 1.5/day held (max +35) - high ROI potential (max -25) + UNDERPERFORMER tag (+20) - STAR tag (-15) + above avg hold for category (+15) + stale category no sales 30d (+10) + low margin <15% (+10) - recently listed <3d (-10). Level: HIGH ≥70, MEDIUM 40-69, LOW <40. Pure compute, no AI/LLM klici. (2) **Context fetch** — `getSellPriorityForHeldTrades()` fetch-a vse held trades + sold trades za per-category context (expectedROI, avgHoldDays, lastSoldDate). (3) **API** — `/api/analytics/sell-priority` GET vrača `SellPriorityList` z `highPriority`, `mediumPriority`, `lowPriority`, `top3`. (4) **TradeRow priority badge** — za held trades prikaže 🔥/🟡/🟢 + score + daysHeld. Hover tooltip prikaže vse razloge + recommendedAction. Color-coded: red=HIGH, amber=MEDIUM, green=LOW. (5) **Sort opcija 'Prioriteta ↓'** — nov option v sort dropdown. Sorts by score desc. (6) **Held Inventory Action Panel** — na vrhu Skladišča ko je filter=held: 'Top 3 za prodajo' card z 3 priority trades. Vsaka kartica: naslov, score badge, daysHeld + buyPrice + tags, recommendedAction (italic), 'Uredi' + 'AI Izhod' quick action gumbi. Color-coded by level. 'Sortiraj po prioriteti' button na vrhu za hit sort. Auto-fetch priority na mount + re-fetch ko trades spremenijo (after add/sell/delete). LIVE: 5 held trades — Sony A7III camera (80 HIGH, 50 dni), Xbox Series X (80 HIGH, 40 dni), Nike Air Max 97 (78 HIGH, 32 dni), PlayStation 5, Stanley ketan set. Razlogi: 'Nad povprečjem za kategorijo (25 dni)' + '50 dni v skladišču'. RecommendedAction: 'Aktivno oglašuj in pripravi na prodajo.' > **v8.43.0 — 📄 Tax Report PDF Export + Annual Summary (Polish phase continues — "Yearly profit/loss breakdown + davčno poročilo PDF za FURS/računovodstvo"):** Problem: Tax report API (v6.9) vrača JSON + CSV, ampak za FURS/računovodstvo RABI PDF. Ni letnega povzetka na Dashboardu — uporabnik mora ročno v Statistike. v8.43 dodaja: (1) **Annual Summary compute module** — `src/lib/trades/annual-summary.ts` (NEW, ~440 vrstic) — `getAnnualSummary(year)` vrača `AnnualSummary` objekt z: (a) **summary** (totalTrades, soldTrades, heldTrades, cancelledTrades, totalRevenue, totalCost, totalBuyCost, totalFees, grossProfit, estimatedTax = grossProfit × 22% Slovenian flat tax, netProfitAfterTax, taxRate, avgROI, winRate, avgHoldDays, avgProfitPerTrade, bestMonth, worstMonth, bestCategory); (b) **quarterly** [4 entries] (Q1-Q4 z tradeCount + revenue + cost + profit + avgROI + winRate); (c) **monthly** [12 entries] (Jan-Dec z tradeCount + profit + cumulativeProfit — vključuje mesece z 0 prodajami); (d) **topTrades** [top 5 by profit]; (e) **worstTrades** [najslabši 3 — samo če so izgube]; (f) **categoryBreakdown** (profit, revenue, cost, roi, winRate per kategorija); (g) **sourceBreakdown** (profit per platforma — Bolha, Vinted, Avtonet, mobile.de). Source derivation: prefer sellLocation, fallback buyLocation, else 'Neznano'. Tax model: 22% poenostavljena stopnja (dohodek iz dejavnosti — ZDoh-2). Pure compute + DB bralno. (2) **Annual Summary API** — `src/app/api/trades/annual-summary/route.ts` (NEW) — GET `?year=2026` → AnnualSummary. 10-min cache (yearly data redko se spreminja). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30s. (3) **Tax Report PDF** — `src/app/api/trades/tax-report-pdf/route.ts` (NEW) — GET `?year=2026` → `application/pdf` binary download z `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="davcno-porocilo-2026.pdf"`. Generirano z **pdfkit** (pure JS PDF library) + **DejaVu Sans** TTF font (bundled v `/assets/fonts/` — full Slovenian char support č/š/ž/ć/đ). 6 sekcij v PDF-ju: (1) Povzetek (12 metrics z bold za bruto dobiček + davek + neto), (2) Četrtletni pregled (Q1-Q4 tabela z zebra striping), (3) Mesečni pregled (Jan-Dec z kumulativo), (4) Top 5 transakcij (z naslov, kategorija, vir, datum, ROI, dobiček), (5) Kategorijski pregled, (6) Viri prihodka (platforme). Footer: "Generirano z Markec AI Firm v8.43 · [timestamp] · Vir: /api/trades/annual-summary?year=YYYY" + "Davek: 22% poenostavljena stopnja (ZDoh-2). Nad 60.000€ letno se zahteva registracija s.p." runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30s. (4) **Annual Summary Dashboard Card** — `src/components/dashboard/annual-summary-card.tsx` (NEW, ~560 vrstic) — 'use client' z self-fetch `/api/trades/annual-summary?year=YYYY` vsakih 60s. Prikazuje: (a) Year selector (currentYear-1, currentYear, currentYear+1); (b) Big profit number (zelena/rdeča); (c) Tax estimate (22%); (d) Net after tax (zelena/rdeča); (e) Quarterly BarChart (Q1-Q4, najboljši=zelena, izguba=rdeča, ostalo=gray); (f) Best/worst month z arrow icon; (g) Win rate + avg ROI; (h) Top trade (z badge za kategorijo); (i) "📄 Prenesi PDF" button → `window.open('/api/trades/tax-report-pdf?year=YYYY')` (browser download); (j) "Celoten pregled" button → navigate to Analitika. Empty state: "Ni prodaj v letu YYYY." (5) **Dashboard Integration** — `src/components/dashboard/dashboard-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — `<AnnualSummaryCard onNavigate={onNavigate} />` dodan po `<WeeklySummaryCard />` (v8.41). Flow: GoalTracker → Trade Stats → Deal Calculator → Profit Timeline → Trade Insights → Weekly Summary → **Annual Summary** → Skladišče. > **v8.40.0 — 📊 Trade Insights Deep Dive (Polish phase continues — "KDaj in KJE prodati za maksimalen profit?"):** Problem: 25 realnih trade-ov (973€ profit v 90d) je v DB, ampak uporabnik nima deep analytics. Ne ve: kateri dan v tednu proda najbolje? Kateri vir (Bolha/Vinted/Avtonet/mobile.de) ima najvišji ROI? Koliko dni naj holdam item? Katera kategorija performa najbolje? Kako so porazdeljeni profiti? v8.40 dodaja pure compute module ki agregira Trade data v 6 actionable insights + Dashboard card z 4 recharts charts. (1) **Trade Insights Compute Module** — `src/lib/trades/trade-insights.ts` (NEW, ~596 vrstic) — `getTradeInsights(days=365)` vrača `TradeInsights` objekt z 6 analizami: (a) **Day-of-Week** (7 vnosov, Ned-Sob) — za vsak dan: tradeCount (sold), totalProfit, avgProfit, sellThroughRate (sold/total za trades BOUGHT tisti dan); bestDayOfWeek + worstDayOfWeek z najvišjim/najnižjim avgProfit. (b) **Source Platform** (group by buyLocation) — za vsak vir: tradeCount, totalInvested (buyPrice+buyFees), totalRevenue (sellPrice), totalProfit, avgROI (profit/invested×100), avgHoldDays, winRate (profitable/total×100), bestCategory (category z najvišjim total profit v tem viru). (c) **Category** (group by category) — tradeCount, totalProfit, avgProfit, avgROI, avgHoldDays, winRate, **trend** (GROWING/STABLE/DECLINING — first half vs second half by sellDate, ×1.1/×0.9 threshold, isti pattern kot v8.37 profit-timeline). (d) **Hold Period** (5 buckets: 0-7d, 8-14d, 15-30d, 31-60d, 60+d) — za vsak bucket: tradeCount, totalProfit, avgProfit, avgROI, winRate; **optimalHoldDays** = bucket z najvišjim avgProfit. (e) **Profit Distribution** (6 buckets: <-50€, -50-0€, 0-50€, 50-100€, 100-200€, 200+€) — tradeCount + percentage; pove ali večina prihodka prihaja iz majhnih winov ali velikih winov. (f) **Actionable Insights** (avtomatska slovenska priporočila) — npr. "📊 Najboljši dan za prodajo: Sobota (85€/trade)", "🏪 Najboljši vir: Vinted (86% ROI, 100% win rate)", "📦 Najboljša kategorija: oblačila (86% ROI, → STABLE)", "⏱️ Optimalni hold: 15-30d (avg 52€/trade, 94% win)", "✅ Win rate: 95% — 18 od 19 donosnih", "💰 Večina trade-ov donosi 0-50€ (53%) (majhni wini prevladujejo)", "⚠️ Win rate pod 50% — preglej pricing strategijo" (samo ko winRate<50). Pure compute, no AI/LLM klici. (2) **Trade Insights API** — `src/app/api/analytics/trade-insights/route.ts` (NEW) — GET `?days=365` → `TradeInsights` (summary + 6 analiz + actionableInsights). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30s. 5-min cache (keyed by days). (3) **Trade Insights Dashboard Card** — `src/components/dashboard/trade-insights-card.tsx` (NEW, ~600 vrstic) — 'use client' z 6 collapsible accordion sections + 4 recharts charts: (a) **Actionable Insights** (vedno visible na vrhu, prominent) — list z ikonami. (b) **Day-of-Week BarChart** — X-axis: Ned/Pon/Tor/Sre/Čet/Pet/Sob, Y-axis: avg profit; best dan = zelena, worst dan = rdeča, ostali = siva. (c) **Source Platform** — BarChart (ROI per source z color-coding: zelena >20%, amber 10-20%, rdeča <10%) + sortable tabela (vir, # trade-ov, investirano, profit, ROI, win rate, hold, best category). (d) **Category** — horizontal BarChart (avg ROI per category z istim color coding) + trend badges (↗ Raste / → Stabilno / ↘ Pada). (e) **Hold Period** — BarChart (avg profit per bucket, optimalni bucket = zelena). (f) **Profit Distribution** — PieChart z 6 segments (rdeče za izgube, amber za small wins, zelena za big wins) + legend z count + %. Summary row (4 mini-stats: skupaj profit, avg ROI, win rate, avg hold). Auto-refresh vsakih 60s. Empty state: "Ni dovolj prodaj za analizo. Dodaj vsaj 5 sold trades." (4) **Dashboard Integration** — `src/components/dashboard/dashboard-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — `<TradeInsightsCard />` dodan po `<ProfitTimelineChart />` (v8.37) in pred Skladišče widget. Flow: GoalTracker → Trade Stats → Deal Calculator → Profit Timeline → **Trade Insights**. Uporabnik ne more vizualno videti napredka proti mesečnemu cilju. v8.39 dodaja: (1) **Goal Tracker Dashboard Card** — `src/components/dashboard/goal-tracker-card.tsx` (NEW) — visual progress bar 0-100% z color coding (red <25%, amber 25-75%, green ≥75%), 4 milestones (25%/50%/75%/100%) kot filled circles (zelena ✓ ko achieved, siva številka ko pending), big numbers "306€ / 500€" (current / goal), projected profit "Projiciran: 678€ (135%)" z trend arrow, days remaining "17 dni do konca meseca" + daily needed "11€/dan potrebnih", MoM trend "↗️ +15% vs prejšnji mesec" ali "↘️ -54% vs prejšnji mesec" z last-month EUR prikazanim, goal status badge (🟡 Na poti / 🟢 Dosežen! / 🔴 Za ciljem), "✏️ Uredi cilj" button → inline edit mode z input field + Save button, "Osveži" button, auto-refresh vsakih 60s. Handles BOTH states: (a) disabled (monthlyGoal=0) prikaže "Nastavi mesečni cilj" prompt z input field + Save button, (b) enabled (monthlyGoal>0) prikaže full UI z progress bar + milestones + projected + daily needed + recommendation + 6-mesečni history mini chart. Replaces the v6.7 inline goal card (ki je bil pogojno render-an samo ko monthlyGoal>0 — disabled state ni imel UI). (2) **Set Goal API** — `src/app/api/trades/goal-tracker/set/route.ts` (NEW) — POST `{monthlyGoal: number}` → `db.settings.update({where:{id:'singleton'}, data:{monthlyProfitGoal:num}})`. Validacija: ne-negativno število, max 1.000.000€. Če goal>0 in current realizedProfit >= goal, kreira Notification (v8.38) `{type:'system', severity:'success', source:'system', title:'🎉 Dosežen mesečni cilj!', body: 'Mesečni cilj ${num}€ je dosežen (realizirani dobiček ${realized}€).', metadata:{monthlyGoal, realizedProfit, achievedAt, sourceEndpoint}}` v try/catch (non-critical — notification failure ne blokira goal update). Returns `{ok:true, monthlyGoal, goalAchieved:boolean}`. runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic'. (3) **Settings UI Integration** — `src/components/dashboard/settings-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — enhanced v4.2 "Mesečni cilj zaslužka" card z v8.39 badge + NOVA `ProfitGoalSection` komponenta (~290 vrstic): input field za monthlyProfitGoal + "Shrani cilj takoj" button ki POST-a na `/api/trades/goal-tracker/set` (NE /api/settings — dedicated endpoint triggerja notification creation ko dosežeš cilj) + live preview box ki fetch-a `/api/trades/goal-tracker` vsakih 60s in prikazuje: status badge, big numbers (realizirano/cilj), progress bar z projected overlay, 4 mini-stats (projiciran dobiček z %, dnevno potrebnih EUR, dni do konca meseca, MoM trend z last-month EUR), recommendation box z color-coded background. "Ne-shranjena sprememba" amber warning ko je draft dirty. Empty state: "⚠️ Mesečni cilj ni nastavljen." Global "Shrani" button še vedno shrani monthlyProfitGoal preko /api/settings (backward compat — ne zlomimo obstoječe funkcionalnosti). (4) **Dashboard Integration** — `src/components/dashboard/dashboard-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — `<GoalTrackerCard />` dodan na vrh dashboard-a (po Customize mode info, pred Activity feed) — goal tracking je najpomembnejša metrika. REMOVED: inline v6.7 goal card (~120 vrstic) + goalData state + useEffect ki je fetch-al goal-tracker na mount (premiščeno v GoalTrackerCard komponento ki self-fetch-a vsakih 60s). (2) **Master Brain orchestration test** (`master-brain.test.ts`) — 9 testov: orchestrates all 7 domain brains (domainSummary.length=7, topActions.length=5), conflicts array, overallHealth score 0-100 z LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL riskLevel, strategy projections (30d/90d/12m profitEUR > 0), oneLineSummary (>20 chars), skip flags (skipProfit → domains.profit=null, domainSummary.length=6), all 7 raw brain results exposed, bottlenecks+strengths arrays, topActions ranked 1..5 z monotonically decreasing finalScore. (4) **Scenario Brain test** (`scenario.test.ts`) — 8 testov: 3 preset scenarios returned, comparisonTable ≥6 rows, aggressive > conservative 12m projection, custom overrides supported, preset configs (capitalMultiplier 0.7/1.0/1.5), 3 scenarios per metric row, full MasterBrainResult attached, recommendation reasoning references bestScenario. Backfill cron izračuna actualProfit30d/90d za vsak snapshot (>= 30 dni star) iz Trade tabele, nato accuracy30d/90d = actual / predicted × 100. UI: 'Master Brain accuracy: 89% (zadnjih 30 dni)' + trend chart (overallHealth progression + 7 domain grade trends: C → B → A) + backfill button za manual test. S v8.25 je Validation phase ZAKLJUČENA: v8.23 (snapshots+actual) + v8.24 (personalization) + v8.25 (accuracy+trend) = uporabnik lahko končno zaupa Master Brain-u z dokazi. *Prej (v8.24.0):* User Risk Profile (personalization): Master Brain do v8.23 priporoča ENAKO strategijo za konzervativnega in agresivnega traderja — to je impersonalno in narobe. v8.24 dodaja UserRiskProfile (riskTolerance, maxAcceptableRisk, liquidityReserve, investmentHorizon) shranjen v Settings tabeli. Master Brain sedaj prilagodi priporočila: conservative profil filtrira HIGH-risk akcije + priporoča REDUCE_RISK če overallHealth < 70; aggressive profil dovoli HIGH-risk akcije + priporoča ACCEPT_RISK če overallHealth > 40. UI: nov 'Tvoj Risk Profile' card z 3 toggle buttons (Conservative/Balanced/Aggressive) + slider za maxAcceptableRisk + input za liquidityReserve. *Prej (v8.23.0):* NEW PHASE: Validation ("Ali lahko zaupaš Master Brain-u?"):** Master Brain daje napovedi ("30d: 3133€"), ampak do v8.23 ni bilo načina za preverbo ali so te napovedi točne. v8.23 dodaja DVE temeljni komponenti za validation: (1) **📊 Actual Profit Tracker** — čista funkcija `calculateActualProfit(days)` ki bere iz Trade tabele (status='sold', sellDate v zadnjih N dneh) in računa DEJANSKI profit: totalProfitEUR, avgMarginPct, dailyAvgEUR, bestTrade, worstTrade. GROUND TRUTH ki ga do sedaj ni bilo. (2) **📸 Daily Brain Snapshots** — cron job @ 00:00 kliče `masterBrain()` in shrani FULL output v novo `BrainSnapshot` Prisma model (overallHealth, 7 domain grades, topActions, conflicts, strategy projections). Temelj za v8.25 (Historical Accuracy). UI: dva nova card-a v Brain view: '📊 Dejanski profit' na vrhu (nad Master Brain banner-jem — ground truth first) + '📸 Brain Snapshots' na dnu (zgodovina z option za manual save). *Prej (v8.22.0):* Brain architecture COMPLETE (7 Domain + 1 Master = 8 layers, 42+ signals → 1 decision). *Sedaj (v8.29.0):* 🎯 INTELLIGENCE PHASE COMPLETE — Master Brain daje KAJ (v8.22), Risk Profile naredi PERSONAL (v8.24), Explainability daje ZAKAJ (v8.26), Scenario Brain odgovarja WHAT IF? (v8.27), Adaptive Weights vzpostavi FEEDBACK LOOP (v8.28), Draft Queue zaključi z ACTION (v8.29 — vsako priporočilo ima sledljivo ✅/❌ odločitev ki neposredno kliče recordActionFeedback).
> **Local-first** — vsi podatki ostanejo na tvojem računalniku. **Zero-cloud**. **0 vulnerabilities**. **129 tests**.

## 🎯 Overview

**Markec AI Firm** je celovit AI sistem za avtomatizirano iskanje, ocenjevanje, kupovanje in preprodajo
oglasev na slovenskih (in srednjeevropskih) oglasnih platformah. Aplikacija teče lokalno na tvojem
računalniku — brez cloud storitev, brez mesečnih naročnin, brez deljenja podatkov z zunanjimi strežniki.

🧠✨ **AI Brain (v8.15–v8.33):** 7 Domain Brains (Profit, Inventory, Market, Sourcing, Risk, Buyer, Pricing) + 1 Master Brain ki orkestrira vseh 7 v ENO končno odločitev (TOP 5 akcij za danes + 30d/90d/12m strategija + conflict resolution med Brain-i + overallHealth score). Profit Brain (v8.15) sintetizira 6 profit signalov. Inventory Brain (v8.16) sintetizira 6 inventory signalov. Market Brain (v8.17) sintetizira 6 market signalov. Sourcing Brain (v8.18) sintetizira 6 sourcing signalov. Risk Brain (v8.19) sintetizira 6 risk signalov. Buyer Brain (v8.20) sintetizira 6 buyer signalov. Pricing Brain (v8.21) sintetizira 6 pricing signalov. **Master Brain (v8.22) — FINAL layer:** pokliče vseh 7 Domain Brain-ov vzporedno, ranked TOP 5 akcij, zazna konflikte, overallHealth 0-100, 30d/90d/12m strategija. **v8.23 — NEW PHASE: Validation:** 📊 Actual Profit Tracker (čita Trade tabelo, računa DEJANSKI profit v zadnjih N dneh — ground truth) + 📸 Daily Brain Snapshots (cron @ 00:00 shrani Master Brain output v novo `BrainSnapshot` Prisma model — temelj za v8.25 Historical Accuracy). **v8.24 — Personalization:** ⚙️ User Risk Profile (conservative/balanced/aggressive) prilagaja Master Brain priporočila glede na uporabnikovo toleranco do tveganja. **v8.25 — Historical Accuracy + Trend (Validation phase CULMINATION):** 📈 Backfill cron izračuna actualProfit30d/90d za vsak BrainSnapshot (>= 30/90 dni star) iz Trade tabele, nato accuracy30d/90d = actual / predicted × 100 — uporabnik lahko končno zaupa Master Brain-u z DEJANSKIMI podatki ("Master Brain accuracy: 89% zadnjih 30 dni"). **v8.26 — ℹ️ Action Explainability (Intelligence phase STARTED):** Za vsako TOP 5 akcijo generira human-readable reasoning (signal/domain trigger + signal score+grade + whyRankedHere + profileImpact + conflictImpact + expectedOutcome) + trustScore (0-100 per akcijo + overall). UI: "ℹ️ Zakaj?" toggle button za vsako akcijo + overall trustScore pill v Master Brain banner-ju ("Trust: 67/100"). **v8.27 — 🎯 Scenario Brain (Intelligence phase continues — WHAT IF?):** Generira 3 preset scenarije (konzervativni -30% kapitala / uravnovešeni default / agresivni +50% kapitala) in za vsakega požene Master Brain vzporedno (3× Promise.all). Vrne comparison table (projiciran profit 30d/90d/12m, overallHealth, riskLevel, top akcija, capital potreben, konflikti) + recommendation (scenario z najvišjim 12m profitom). POST podpira custom "what-if" inpute (capitalDeployed, tradesPerMonth, riskTolerance). UI: 🎯 Scenario Brain card (rose/pink) z comparison table + recommendation banner + custom form. **v8.28 — 🎛️ Adaptive Domain Weights (Intelligence phase continues — FEEDBACK LOOP):** Master Brain (v8.22) je uporabljal HARDCODED domain weights (risk=1.3, profit=1.2, ...). v8.28 naredi uteži ADAPTIVE — shranjene v Settings.adaptiveDomainWeights (JSON), Master Brain jih naloži namesto hardcoded. Feedback loop: ko uporabnik označi akcijo kot 'executed' ali 'rejected', sistem beleži per-domain execution stats. Po vsakih 10 akcijah per domeno: executionRate > 0.8 → weight × 1.1 (boost), < 0.4 → × 0.9 (reduce), clamp [0.5, 2.0]. UI: 🎛️ Adaptive Weights card (bright orange) z 7 sliderji (0.5-2.0) + execution stats + rate bars + adjustment history + reset/manual override buttons + feedback demo form. **v8.29 — 📋 Draft Queue + Action Feedback Loop integration (🎯 INTELLIGENCE PHASE COMPLETE):** Master Brain banner dobi ✅ Izvedel / ❌ Zavrnil gumbe za vsako TOP 5 akcijo. Klik shranjuje odločitev v NOV `ActionDraft` Prisma model (status pending/approved/executed/rejected/expired) IN avtomatsko kliče `recordActionFeedback()` iz v8.28 — adaptive weights se naučijo iz REVEALED preference uporabnika. UI: 📋 Draft Queue card (slate/blue-gray) z zgodovino (zadnjih 30 draftov) + filter (status/domain) + per-domain execution rates + cleanup button (cron briše >90 dni stare). **v8.30 — 🤖 Safe Auto-pilot (NEW PHASE: Automation STARTED):** Samodejno izvaja SAMO LOW-risk akcije (confidence=LOW, uplift<100€, domain!=risk, daily limit≤5, budget≤500€) ki izpolnjujejo VSA 8 varnostna pravila. MEDIUM/HIGH risk akcije zahtevajo ročni ✅ Izvedel klik (v8.29). Rollback: vsako auto-executed akcijo lahko uporabnik razveljavi (↩️ Razveljavi) — to tudi kliče recordActionFeedback z 'rejected' da undo-a learning. UI: 🤖 Auto-pilot card (purple/indigo) z master switch + config sliders + stats + history z rollback + safety info box. Cron: hourly cron preverja eligible drafts. **v8.31 — 🚀 Aggressive Auto-pilot + Anomaly Detection (🎯 AUTOMATION PHASE COMPLETE):** Aggressive mode (opt-in, double confirmation) dovoli tudi MEDIUM confidence (ne le LOW), višji limiti (10/dan, 2000€/dan), uplift<300€. HIGH je še vedno vedno manual (oba modal). Domain='risk' izključen (oba). Anomaly detection: če >8 akcij v 1 uri → suspendiran (possible loop), zahteva manual re-enable preko POST {action:'clear_anomaly'}. Double opt-in za aggressive: prvi klik nastavi pending (5-min window), drugi klik znotraj 5 minut potrdi. UI: mode selector z double-click confirmation + anomaly banner (red) + threshold comparison box (Safe vs Aggressive). **v8.32 — 🏥 System Health Dashboard (NEW PHASE: Polish STARTED — "How healthy is the Brain system?"):** One endpoint ki agregira celotno Brain sistem health: 8 brain endpoints status (responsive + responseTimeMs + grade), data freshness (latest snapshot, trades count, accuracy30d), auto-pilot status (enabled, mode, anomalySuspended, today's stats), draft queue summary (pending/executed/rejected/expired + execution rate), risk profile, adaptive weights summary, in auto-generated recommendations (5+ rules). Overall health score 0-100 (40% brains responsive + 20% data freshness + 15% draft queue + 15% auto-pilot + 10% risk profile). UI: 🏥 System Health card na VRHU Brain view (health overview first) z gradient background (emerald HEALTHY / amber DEGRADED / red UNHEALTHY) + 8 brain endpoints grid (2 rows × 4 cols z status dot + response time + grade) + data freshness + auto-pilot + draft queue + recommendations list + 🔄 Osveži button z 60s auto-refresh. **🎯 Brain architecture COMPLETE (v8.22) + Validation phase ZAKLJUČENA (v8.23+v8.24+v8.25) + Intelligence phase ZAKLJUČENA (v8.26+v8.27+v8.28+v8.29) + Automation phase ZAKLJUČENA (v8.30 Safe + v8.31 Aggressive+Anomaly) + Polish phase STARTED (v8.32 System Health Dashboard) — 7 Domain + 1 Master = 8 brain layers, 42+ signals → 1 decision, sedaj z ground truth tracking, historical snapshots, personal risk profile, historical accuracy %, per-action explainability (ZAKAJ), scenario simulation (WHAT IF?), adaptive feedback loop (LEARNING), draft queue akcijske odločitve (ACTION — closed feedback loop), mode-aware auto-pilot za LOW/MEDIUM-risk akcije z anomaly detection (AUTOMATION — daj mi action, ne le priporočilo) IN unified system health dashboard (MONITORING — ali je sistem zdrav?).**

### Čisto v eni povedi
Lovi podcenjene oglase na Bolhi/Facebooku/Vintedu z AI, jih kupi poceni, preprodaj drago z
AI-optimiziranimi oglasi, in avtomatiziraj celoten workflow od odkritja do prodaje.

### Verzija v9.28.0 (avgust 2026)

**432 AI endpointov** + **84 analytics** + **18 cron automatizacij** + **11 Telegram ukazov** + **11 platform** + **193 modulov** + **3 CI/CD workflow-i** + **158 testov (100% pass)** organiziranih v 18 zavihkov:
- **Statistike** (analytics, predictions, forecasting) — 35+ funkcij
- **Skladišče** (inventory management, aging, depreciation) — 20+ funkcij
- **Oglasi** (listing optimization, SEO, image analysis) — 25+ funkcij
- **Negotiation** (real-time bot, chatbot, playbook) — 8+ funkcij
- **Buyers/Customers** (segmentation, trust score, matching) — 12+ funkcij
- **Risk/Insurance** (hedging, fraud detection, claims) — 10+ funkcij
- **Finance/Profit** (margin, ROI, compounding, tax) — 16+ funkcij

### Kaj je novega v v7.56–v8.45 (89 verzij, 217 novih funkcij)

**v8.45 — 📱 Mobile-First Responsive Optimization + Touch UX (Polish phase — "Mobile bottom nav + FAB + haptic feedback + touch-optimized layouts"):**
- **📱 MOBILE-FIRST RESPONSIVE OPTIMIZATION + TOUCH UX.** Problem: sistem deluje na desktop, ampak mobile UX je suboptimalen — 17 nav gumbov na vrhu je neuporabnih na telefonu, tap targeti so premajhni za palec, ni hitrega dostopa do "Dodaj trade" na mobilu, PWA shortcuts so minimalni. v8.45 dodaja: (1) **Mobile bottom navigation bar** — `src/components/dashboard/mobile-bottom-nav.tsx` (NEW) — fixed bottom bar z 5 najpomembnejšimi sekcijami (Dashboard, AI Hub, Trades z badge za held count, Alerti z unread count badge, Nastavitve). `md:hidden` — prikazuje se samo na mobilu. 44×44px min touch targeti, `bg-background/95 backdrop-blur-md border-t`, safe-area-inset-bottom support (iOS). Active state: highlighted icon + label + top indicator bar. Haptic feedback (`navigator.vibrate(10)`) na tap. Fetches held count iz `/api/trades/dashboard` vsakih 60s za Trades badge. (2) **Floating Action Button (FAB)** — `src/components/dashboard/mobile-fab.tsx` (NEW) — `fixed bottom-20 right-4 z-50`, purple→primary gradient (`bg-gradient-to-br from-primary to-purple-600`), 56×56px, pulsing glow animation (`fab-pulse-glow` keyframe v globals.css — expanding box-shadow ring, `prefers-reduced-motion` respected). On tap: `haptic.medium()` + odpre QuickAddTradeModal (v8.36). `md:hidden`. (3) **Haptic feedback hook** — `src/hooks/use-haptic.ts` (NEW) — `useHaptic()` vrača 6 vibracijskih patternov: `light` (10ms — nav taps), `medium` (20ms — primary actions), `success` ([10,30,10] — save), `error` ([20,50,20,50,20] — failure), `selection` (5ms — item pick), `warning` (30ms — caution). Safe guards za SSR + unsupported browsers. (4) **Touch-optimized dashboard layouts** — `src/components/dashboard/dashboard-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — Danes card numbers `text-xl` → `text-2xl sm:text-3xl` (večji na mobilu); StatCard numbers `text-2xl` → `text-2xl sm:text-3xl`; filter chips `px-3 py-1` → `px-3 py-2.5 sm:py-1 min-h-[40px] sm:min-h-0` (40px tap target na mobilu); quick action bar buttons `min-h-[44px] sm:min-h-0` (44px HIG/Material minimum); recent runs rows `min-h-[48px] sm:min-h-0`; grid breakpoints `grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5` → `grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-5` (boljši tablet layout); loading skeleton `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4` → `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4`. Haptic feedback dodan na vseh 6 filter chipsov + Dodaj trade + Poženi vse monitorje. (5) **PWA shortcuts** — `public/manifest.json` (MODIFIED) — dodani shortcuts: "Dodaj trade" (`/?action=add-trade`), "AI Hub" (`/?view=ai-hub`), "Dashboard" (`/?view=dashboard`), "Alerti" (`/?view=alerts`), "Oglasi" (`/?view=listings`). PWA shortcut handler v page.tsx prebere `?view=` in `?action=` parametre na mount → setView ali odpre QuickAddTradeModal + cleans URL z `replaceState`. (6) **Horizontally scrollable nav** — desktop nav (17 gumbov) dobi `touch-scroll` class (custom scrollbar styling v globals.css) za smoother horizontal scroll na touch devices. (7) **Footer clearance** — outer wrapper `pb-16 md:pb-0` + main `pb-20 md:pb-6` da fixed bottom nav ne prekrije footer/content na mobilu. Stats: AI endpointi 431 (nespremenjeno — UI only, no new API), Total API routes 623 (nespremenjeno), funkcije ~305→~307 (+2: MobileBottomNav + MobileFAB; useHaptic hook je utility). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Agent Browser verification (375×812 iPhone X): bottom nav visible z 5 gumbi + Trades badge "5" (held count) ✅, FAB visible 56×56px bottom-right ✅, tap "Nastavitve" navigira na Settings view ✅. Desktop (1920×1080): bottom nav `display:none` ✅, FAB `display:none` ✅. `curl /api/ai-list` → `{ok:true, total:431}` (unchanged) ✅. **Polish phase continues — v8.32 (monitoring) + v8.33 (performance) + v8.34 (testing) + v8.35 (data+telegram) + v8.36 (CSV Import) + v8.37 (Deal Calculator) + v8.38 (Notification Center) + v8.39 (Goal Tracker) + v8.40 (Trade Insights) + v8.41 (Weekly Summary) + v8.42 (Backup) + v8.43 (Tax Report PDF) + v8.44 (Smart Restock) + v8.45 (Mobile-First Responsive + Touch UX).**

**v8.43 — 📄 Tax Report PDF Export + Annual Summary (Polish phase — "Yearly profit/loss breakdown + davčno poročilo PDF za FURS/računovodstvo"):**
- **📄 TAX REPORT PDF EXPORT + ANNUAL SUMMARY (POLISH PHASE — YEARLY P&L BREAKDOWN + DAVČNO POROČILO PDF ZA FURS/RAČUNOVODSTVO Z 6 SEKCIJAMI).** Problem: Tax report API (v6.9) vrača JSON + CSV, ampak za FURS/računovodstvo RABI PDF. Ni letnega povzetka na Dashboardu — uporabnik mora ročno v Statistike. v8.43 dodaja: (1) **Annual Summary compute module** — `src/lib/trades/annual-summary.ts` (NEW, ~440 vrstic) — `getAnnualSummary(year)` vrača `AnnualSummary` objekt z: (a) **summary** (totalTrades, soldTrades, heldTrades, cancelledTrades, totalRevenue, totalCost, totalBuyCost, totalFees, grossProfit, estimatedTax = grossProfit × 22% Slovenian flat tax, netProfitAfterTax, taxRate, avgROI, winRate, avgHoldDays, avgProfitPerTrade, bestMonth, worstMonth, bestCategory); (b) **quarterly** [4 entries] (Q1-Q4 z tradeCount + revenue + cost + profit + avgROI + winRate); (c) **monthly** [12 entries] (Jan-Dec z tradeCount + profit + cumulativeProfit — vključuje mesece z 0 prodajami); (d) **topTrades** [top 5 by profit]; (e) **worstTrades** [najslabši 3 — samo če so izgube]; (f) **categoryBreakdown** (profit, revenue, cost, roi, winRate per kategorija); (g) **sourceBreakdown** (profit per platforma — Bolha, Vinted, Avtonet, mobile.de). Source derivation: prefer sellLocation, fallback buyLocation, else 'Neznano'. Tax model: 22% poenostavljena stopnja (dohodek iz dejavnosti — ZDoh-2). Pure compute + DB bralno. (2) **Annual Summary API** — `src/app/api/trades/annual-summary/route.ts` (NEW) — GET `?year=2026` → AnnualSummary. 10-min cache (yearly data redko se spreminja). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30s. (3) **Tax Report PDF** — `src/app/api/trades/tax-report-pdf/route.ts` (NEW) — GET `?year=2026` → `application/pdf` binary download z `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="davcno-porocilo-2026.pdf"`. Generirano z **pdfkit** (pure JS PDF library, NEW dependency) + **DejaVu Sans** TTF font (bundled v `/assets/fonts/` — full Slovenian char support č/š/ž/ć/đ). 6 sekcij v PDF-ju: (1) Povzetek (12 metrics z bold za bruto dobiček + davek + neto), (2) Četrtletni pregled (Q1-Q4 tabela z zebra striping), (3) Mesečni pregled (Jan-Dec z kumulativo), (4) Top 5 transakcij (z naslov, kategorija, vir, datum, ROI, dobiček), (5) Kategorijski pregled, (6) Viri prihodka (platforme). Footer: "Generirano z Markec AI Firm v8.43 · [timestamp]" + "Davek: 22% poenostavljena stopnja (ZDoh-2). Nad 60.000€ letno se zahteva registracija s.p." runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30s. (4) **Annual Summary Dashboard Card** — `src/components/dashboard/annual-summary-card.tsx` (NEW, ~560 vrstic) — 'use client' z self-fetch `/api/trades/annual-summary?year=YYYY` vsakih 60s. Prikazuje: (a) Year selector (currentYear-1, currentYear, currentYear+1); (b) Big profit number (zelena/rdeča); (c) Tax estimate (22%); (d) Net after tax (zelena/rdeča); (e) Quarterly BarChart (Q1-Q4, najboljši=zelena, izguba=rdeča, ostalo=gray); (f) Best/worst month z arrow icon; (g) Win rate + avg ROI; (h) Top trade (z badge za kategorijo); (i) "📄 Prenesi PDF" button → `window.open('/api/trades/tax-report-pdf?year=YYYY')` (browser download); (j) "Celoten pregled" button → navigate to Analitika. Empty state: "Ni prodaj v letu YYYY." (5) **Dashboard Integration** — `src/components/dashboard/dashboard-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — `<AnnualSummaryCard onNavigate={onNavigate} />` dodan po `<WeeklySummaryCard />` (v8.41). Flow: GoalTracker → Trade Stats → Deal Calculator → Profit Timeline → Trade Insights → Weekly Summary → **Annual Summary** → Skladišče. LIVE SUMMARY iz 25 real trades za leto 2026: 19 sold + 5 held + 1 cancelled, 973€ gross profit, 214.06€ estimated tax, 758.94€ net after tax, 94.74% win rate, 34.65% avg ROI, 21.84 avg hold days, 51.21€ avg profit/trade. Best month: Julij (667€). Worst month: Avgust (306€). Best category: elektronika (377€). Top 5 trades: Alu platišča 17" (+145€), MacBook Air M1 (+115€), iPhone 13 128GB (+85€), iPad Air 2022 (+77€), Zimske gume 205/55 (+70€). Source breakdown: Bolha (11 trades, 541€), Avtonet (3, 242€), mobile.de (2, 111€), Vinted (3, 79€). PDF: 3 strani, 32KB, full Slovenian char support. Stats: AI endpointi 430 (nespremenjeno — annual-summary + tax-report-pdf so pod /api/trades/, ne /api/ai/), Total API routes 620→622 (+2: annual-summary, tax-report-pdf), funkcije ~303→~304 (+1: AnnualSummaryCard). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Endpoint verification: `curl '/api/trades/annual-summary?year=2026'` → 200 z `{ok:true, year:2026, summary:{totalTrades:25, soldTrades:19, grossProfit:973, estimatedTax:214.06, ...}, quarterly:[4], monthly:[12], topTrades:[5], worstTrades:[1], categoryBreakdown:[5], sourceBreakdown:[4], source:'v8.43-annual-summary'}` (3.9 KB JSON) ✅. `curl '/api/trades/tax-report-pdf?year=2026'` → 200 z `application/pdf`, 32KB PDF, Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="davcno-porocilo-2026.pdf" ✅. `curl /api/ai-list` → `{ok:true, total:430}` (unchanged) ✅. **Polish phase continues — v8.32 (monitoring) + v8.33 (performance) + v8.34 (testing) + v8.35 (data+telegram) + v8.36 (CSV Import) + v8.37 (Deal Calculator) + v8.38 (Notification Center) + v8.39 (Goal Tracker) + v8.40 (Trade Insights) + v8.41 (Weekly Summary) + v8.42 (Full System Backup & Restore) + v8.43 (Tax Report PDF Export + Annual Summary — yearly P&L breakdown + davčno poročilo PDF za FURS/računovodstvo z 6 sekcijami).**

**v8.42 — 💾 Full System Backup & Restore (JSON) (Polish phase — "Portable, human-readable backup of ALL 18 tables + 3 restore modes + auto-backup cron"):**
- **💾 FULL SYSTEM BACKUP & RESTORE (JSON) (POLISH PHASE — PORTABLE, HUMAN-READABLE BACKUP OF ALL 18 PRISMA TABLES + 3 RESTORE MODES + AUTO-BACKUP CRON).** Problem: Raw .db backup (v1.3) je binaren (SQLite-specific, ni prenosljiv, ne more pregledati brez SQLite orodja). v4.7 JSON backup je pokrival samo 10 tabel — manjkajo BrainSnapshot, ActionDraft, Notification, Profile, SavedSearch, SmartRule, NegotiationMessage, WebhookEndpoint (vse v5.3+/v8.x dodatki). Ni restore UI. Ni auto-backup cron. Local-first sistem z 25 trades + 1 snapshot + 70 drafts + 4 notifications RABI robusten backup. v8.42 dodaja: (1) **JSON Export module** — `src/lib/backup/backup.ts` (NEW, ~520 vrstic) — `exportBackup()` vrača `BackupData` objekt z `version:'v8.42'`, `createdAt`, `dbVersion`, `tables` (PLURAL ključi: profiles, settings, savedSearches, pushSubscriptions, trades, digestLogs, monitors, listings, alerts, runLogs, heartbeatLogs, priceHistory, smartRules, negotiationMessages, webhookEndpoints, actionDrafts, brainSnapshots, notifications — VSEH 18 tabel) in `stats` (totalRecords + per-table counts za UI preview). Settings občutljiva polja (aiApiKey, fallbackApiKey, telegramBotToken, telegramWebhookSecret, discordWebhookUrl, slackWebhookUrl, emailSmtpPassword, vapidPrivateKey) so REDACTED z `'***REDACTED***'` sentinelom — varno za deljenje/arhiviranje. `getDbStats()` vrača samo counte za UI preview (brez full export). (2) **JSON Restore module** — `restoreBackup(data, options)` v istem modulu — 3 modi: (a) **replace** = deleteMany({}) + create() (total wipe + restore, NEVARNO); (b) **merge** = upsert by id (create if missing, update if exists — priporočeno); (c) **skip** = findUnique + create only if not exists (preserve current rows, najbolj varno). `tables` filter omogoča restore samo določenih tabel. Per-table try/catch — če ena tabela faila, ostale še vedno restore. Settings special: nikoli ne prepiše občutljivih polj z redacted sentinelom (preserve real API keys/tokens). Restore order: independent tables first (Profile, Settings, ...) → dependent last (Listing, Alert, ...) za minimal FK violations. (3) **Backup/Restore API** — `src/app/api/backup/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — GET `?format=json` → `exportBackup()` z `Content-Disposition: attachment` (download). GET `?format=stats` → `getDbStats()` za UI preview. GET `?download=1` (raw .db) in GET (info) ohranjena za backward compat. NEW `src/app/api/backup/restore/route.ts` — POST `{data, mode, tables?}` → `restoreBackup()`, vrača `RestoreResult` z per-table restored/skipped/errors counts. runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=60s. NEW `src/app/api/backup/list/route.ts` — GET → `listBackups()` vrača list `[{filename, sizeKB, createdAt}]` (newest first). (4) **Auto-backup cron** — `src/app/api/cron/auto-backup/route.ts` (NEW) — GET + POST handler. kliče `exportBackup()` → `saveBackupToFile()` (shrani v `/backups/backup-YYYY-MM-DDTHH-MM-SS-mmmZ.json`) → `cleanupOldBackups(30)` (izbriše starejše od 30 najnovejših). Auth: `?key=<MONITOR_CRON_KEY>` (dev mode = no auth). Schedule: daily @ 02:00 (po daily-brain-snapshot @ 00:00 — capture latest BrainSnapshot row). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=60s. (5) **Settings UI — Backup & Restore section** — `src/components/dashboard/settings-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — new `FullBackupSection` component (~580 vrstic) z 5 sekcijami: (a) **Trenutno stanje baze** — grid 12 table counts (profiles, settings, savedSearches, pushSubs, trades, digestLogs, monitors, listings, alerts, runLogs, heartbeatLogs, priceHistory) + total record count + estimated backup size KB; (b) **💾 Export JSON backup** — button → `window.open('/api/backup?format=json')` (browser download); (c) **📥 Restore iz JSON backup** — file picker (.json) + preview (version, createdAt, table counts) + mode selector (3 gumbi: ⚠️ Zamenjaj vse / 📝 Združi / ⏭️ Preskoči) + collapsible table selector (checkboxes za vsako tabelo z record count) + restore button (destructive variant za replace mode) + double confirmation dialog za replace mode ("🚨 ZADNJI POTRDITEV") + result display z per-table restored/skipped counts + collapsible errors section; (d) **📅 Auto-backup (cron)** — "Poženi auto-backup zdaj" button → POST `/api/cron/auto-backup` + recent backups list (newest 10) z download link per file + file size + creation timestamp; (e) **⚠️ Warning box** — "Backup je priporočljiv pred večjimi spremembami. Local-first = tvoji podatki so samo na tem računalniku." Stats: AI endpointi 430 (nespremenjeno — backup routes so pod /api/backup/ in /api/cron/, ne /api/ai/), Total API routes 617→620 (+3: backup/restore, backup/list, cron/auto-backup), funkcije ~302→~303 (+1: FullBackupSection). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. Endpoint verification: `curl '/api/backup?format=json'` → 200 z `{version:'v8.42', createdAt, dbVersion:'v8.38-notification-center', tables:{settings:[1], trades:[25], brainSnapshots:[1], actionDrafts:[70], notifications:[4], ...18 tables}, stats:{totalRecords:101, tableCounts:{...}}}` (105 KB) ✅. `curl '/api/backup?format=stats'` → 200 z `{ok:true, totalRecords:101, tableCounts:{...}, version:'v8.42'}` ✅. `curl '/api/backup/list'` → 200 z `{ok:true, backups:[], count:0}` ✅. `POST /api/cron/auto-backup` → 200 z `{ok:true, saved:{filename, sizeKB:82, path}, stats:{totalRecords:101}, cleanup:{deleted:0, kept:1, total:1}, source:'v8.42-auto-backup'}` (saved to /backups/) ✅. `POST /api/backup/restore {data, mode:'merge', tables:['notifications']}` → 200 z `{ok:true, mode:'merge', restored:{notifications:4}, skipped:{notifications:0}, errors:[], source:'v8.42-restore'}` ✅. `POST /api/backup/restore {data, mode:'skip', tables:['trades','brainSnapshots','notifications']}` → 200 z `{ok:true, mode:'skip', restored:{trades:0, brainSnapshots:0, notifications:0}, skipped:{trades:25, brainSnapshots:1, notifications:4}, errors:[]}` (all skipped — already exist) ✅. `curl /api/ai-list` → `{ok:true, total:430}` (unchanged) ✅. **Polish phase continues — v8.32 (monitoring) + v8.33 (performance) + v8.34 (testing) + v8.35 (data+telegram) + v8.36 (CSV Import) + v8.37 (Deal Calculator) + v8.38 (Notification Center) + v8.39 (Goal Tracker) + v8.40 (Trade Insights) + v8.41 (Weekly Summary) + v8.42 (Full System Backup & Restore — portable, human-readable JSON backup z 3 restore modes + auto-backup cron).**

**v8.41 — 📋 Weekly Summary Report + Email Notifications (Polish phase — "Comprehensive weekly digest"):**
- **📋 WEEKLY SUMMARY REPORT + EMAIL NOTIFICATIONS (POLISH PHASE — COMPREHENSIVE WEEKLY DIGEST SENT TO TELEGRAM + EMAIL + NOTIFICATION CENTER).** Problem: Daily Telegram digest (v8.35) je kratek — samo TOP 5 akcij. Ni tedenskega povzetka. Email.ts (v2.7) obstaja ampak se ne uporablja za Brain sistem. v8.41 dodaja comprehensive weekly digest: (1) **Weekly Summary Compute Module** — `src/lib/brain/weekly-summary.ts` (NEW, ~830 vrstic) — `generateWeeklySummary()` vrača `WeeklySummary` objekt z: (a) **profit** (thisWeek, lastWeek, momChange %, total30d, goalProgress %, goalMonthly, goalRealized); (b) **trades** (soldThisWeek, soldValue, heldCount, avgHoldDays, winRate); (c) **topTrades** (top 3 by profit this week z title + profit + category + source); (d) **worstTrade** (samo če je negativen); (e) **brainHealth** (score + grade + riskLevel + topAction + conflictsCount — klice `masterBrain()` v8.22 v try/catch); (f) **insightsHighlights** (top 3 actionable insights iz v8.40 `getTradeInsights(365)`); (g) **recommendations** (auto-generirana slovenska priporočila za naslednji teden, 2-5 items, glede na profit/MoM/goal/heldCount/Brain top action); (h) **telegramMessage** (plain text, parseMode: null — isti pattern kot v8.35 sendBrainDigest); (i) **emailSubject** + **emailHtml** (styled HTML dark-theme z tabele, isti style kot v6.92 formatAlertEmail z `escapeHtml` XSS zaščito). Period = zadnji končan teden (ponedeljek-nedelja). Pure compute + DB bralno. (2) **Send Weekly Summary** — `sendWeeklySummary()` v istem modulu — vedno (a) generira summary, (b) kreira Notification record (v8.38 Notification Center — `type:'system'`, `severity:'success'` če profit ≥0 ali `warning` če <0, `source:'system'`, metadata z periodStart/End + thisWeekProfit + momChange + goalProgress + soldCount + winRate + brainHealthScore + insightsCount), (c) pošlje na Telegram če konfiguriran (best-effort, non-blocking), (d) pošlje na Email če konfiguriran (best-effort, non-blocking). `ok:true` tudi če noben kanal ni konfiguriran — povzetek je vseeno shranjen v Notification Center. (3) **Enhanced weekly-report cron** — `src/app/api/cron/weekly-report/route.ts` (MODIFIED) — nadomesti v7.38 minimalni Telegram/Discord report z `sendWeeklySummary()`. ISTA pot (`/api/cron/weekly-report`) — backward kompatibilno. Auth: `?key=<MONITOR_CRON_KEY>` (dev mode = no auth). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=60s. GET + POST handler. (4) **Weekly Summary API** — `src/app/api/ai/brain/weekly-summary/route.ts` (NEW) — GET vrača povzetek brez pošiljanja (preview za Dashboard), POST `{action:'send'}` pošlje na Telegram + Email + Notification Center. runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=60s. **v8.40 — 📊 Trade Insights Deep Dive (Polish phase — "KDaj in KJE prodati za maksimalen profit?"):**
- **📊 TRADE INSIGHTS DEEP DIVE (POLISH PHASE — "KDAJ IN KJE PRODATI ZA MAKSIMALEN PROFIT?").** Problem: 25 realnih trade-ov (973€ profit v 90d) je v DB, ampak uporabnik nima deep analytics. Ne ve: kateri dan v tednu proda najbolje? Kateri vir (Bolha/Vinted/Avtonet/mobile.de) ima najvišji ROI? Koliko dni naj holdam item? Katera kategorija performa najbolje? Kako so porazdeljeni profiti (majhni wini vs veliki wini)? v8.40 dodaja pure compute module ki agregira Trade data v 6 actionable insights + Dashboard card z 4 recharts charts. (1) **Trade Insights Compute Module** — `src/lib/trades/trade-insights.ts` (NEW, ~596 vrstic) — `getTradeInsights(days=365)` vrača `TradeInsights` objekt z 6 analizami: (a) **Day-of-Week** (7 vnosov, Ned-Sob) — za vsak dan: tradeCount (sold), totalProfit, avgProfit, sellThroughRate (sold/total za trades BOUGHT tisti dan); bestDayOfWeek + worstDayOfWeek z najvišjim/najnižjim avgProfit. (b) **Source Platform** (group by buyLocation) — za vsak vir: tradeCount, totalInvested (buyPrice+buyFees), totalRevenue (sellPrice), totalProfit, avgROI (profit/invested×100), avgHoldDays, winRate (profitable/total×100), bestCategory (category z najvišjim total profit v tem viru). (c) **Category** (group by category) — tradeCount, totalProfit, avgProfit, avgROI, avgHoldDays, winRate, **trend** (GROWING/STABLE/DECLINING — first half vs second half by sellDate, ×1.1/×0.9 threshold, isti pattern kot v8.37 profit-timeline). (d) **Hold Period** (5 buckets: 0-7d, 8-14d, 15-30d, 31-60d, 60+d) — za vsak bucket: tradeCount, totalProfit, avgProfit, avgROI, winRate; **optimalHoldDays** = bucket z najvišjim avgProfit. (e) **Profit Distribution** (6 buckets: <-50€, -50-0€, 0-50€, 50-100€, 100-200€, 200+€) — tradeCount + percentage; pove ali večina prihodka prihaja iz majhnih winov ali velikih winov. (f) **Actionable Insights** (avtomatska slovenska priporočila) — npr. "📊 Najboljši dan za prodajo: Sobota (85€/trade)", "🏪 Najboljši vir: Vinted (86% ROI, 100% win rate)", "📦 Najboljša kategorija: oblačila (86% ROI, → STABLE)", "⏱️ Optimalni hold: 15-30d (avg 52€/trade, 94% win)", "✅ Win rate: 95% — 18 od 19 donosnih", "💰 Večina trade-ov donosi 0-50€ (53%) (majhni wini prevladujejo)", "⚠️ Win rate pod 50% — preglej pricing strategijo" (samo ko winRate<50). Pure compute, no AI/LLM klici. Per-trade profit formula consistent z v8.23 actual.ts + v8.37 profit-timeline: `profit = sellPrice - sellFees - buyPrice - buyFees`. (2) **Trade Insights API** — `src/app/api/analytics/trade-insights/route.ts` (NEW) — GET `?days=365` → `TradeInsights` (summary + 6 analiz + actionableInsights). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30s. 5-min cache (keyed by days, per v7.32 pattern). (3) **Trade Insights Dashboard Card** — `src/components/dashboard/trade-insights-card.tsx` (NEW, ~600 vrstic) — 'use client' z 6 collapsible accordion sections + 4 recharts charts: (a) **Actionable Insights** (vedno visible na vrhu, prominent) — list z ikonami + Lightbulb icon header. (b) **Day-of-Week BarChart** — X-axis: Ned/Pon/Tor/Sre/Čet/Pet/Sob, Y-axis: avg profit; best dan = zelena, worst dan = rdeča, ostali = siva; tooltip prikaže tradeCount; best/worst summary box pod chart-om. (c) **Source Platform** — BarChart (ROI per source z color-coding: zelena >20%, amber 10-20%, rdeča <10%) + sortable tabela (vir, # trade-ov, investirano, profit, ROI, win rate, hold, best category) z max-h-72 overflow + custom scrollbar. (d) **Category** — horizontal BarChart (avg ROI per category z istim color coding) + trend badges (↗ Raste / → Stabilno / ↘ Pada) za vsako kategorijo. (e) **Hold Period** — BarChart (avg profit per bucket, optimalni bucket = zelena). (f) **Profit Distribution** — PieChart z 6 segments (rdeče za izgube <-50€/-50-0€, amber za small wins 0-50€, zelena za big wins 50-100€/100-200€/200+€) + legend z count + %. Summary row (4 mini-stats: skupaj profit, avg ROI, win rate, avg hold). Auto-refresh vsakih 60s. Empty state: "Ni dovolj prodaj za analizo. Dodaj vsaj 5 sold trades." (4) **Dashboard Integration** — `src/components/dashboard/dashboard-view.tsx` (MODIFIED) — `<TradeInsightsCard />` dodan po `<ProfitTimelineChart />` (v8.37) in pred Skladišče widget. Dashboard flow: GoalTracker → Trade Stats → Deal Calculator → Profit Timeline → **Trade Insights** → Skladišče. Curl test: `curl /api/analytics/trade-insights?days=365` → `{ok:true, summary:{totalTrades:25, soldTrades:19, heldTrades:5, cancelledTrades:1, totalProfit:973, totalInvested:2808, avgProfitPerTrade:51.21, avgROI:34.65, avgHoldDays:21.84, overallWinRate:94.74, profitableCount:18}, bestDayOfWeek:{dayName:"Sobota", avgProfit:85}, worstDayOfWeek:{dayName:"Četrtek", avgProfit:27.25}, bestSource:{source:"Vinted", avgROI:85.87, winRate:100, bestCategory:"oblačila"}, bestCategory:{category:"oblačila", avgROI:85.87, trend:"STABLE"}, optimalHoldDays:"15-30d", dayOfWeekAnalysis:[7 entries], sourcePlatformAnalysis:[4 sources: Bolha/Avtonet/mobile.de/Vinted], categoryAnalysis:[5 categories: elektronika/avto/orodje/oblačila/obutev], holdPeriodAnalysis:[5 buckets], profitDistribution:[6 buckets], actionableInsights:[9 Slovenian recommendations]}` ✅. /api/ai-list → total 429 (nespremenjeno — trade-insights je pod /api/analytics/ ne /api/ai/) ✅. **v8.38 — 🔔 Notification Center + Alert History (Polish phase — Centralizirana zgodovina vseh obvestil):**
- **🔔 NOTIFICATION CENTER + ALERT HISTORY (POLISH PHASE — CENTRALIZIRANA ZGODOVINA VSEH OBVESTIL).** Problem: obvestila so razpršena — Telegram messages (Brain digests, auto-pilot executions, anomalies), toast notifications, dev.log. Uporabnik nima centralnega pregleda "kaj se je zgodilo v zadnjih 30 dneh". v8.38 dodaja: (1) NEW `Notification` Prisma model (splošen, ne vezan na Monitor/Listing kot obstoječi `Alert` model — obstoječi je za delivery history telegram/discord/slack/email/push per alert; novi je za Brain system events in vse druge sistemske dogodke). Polja: id, type (8 tipov: brain_digest | autopilot_executed | autopilot_rollback | anomaly | price_drop | system | trade_sold | error), title, body (plain text ali JSON), severity (info | success | warning | error), source (brain | autopilot | telegram | system | manual), isRead, readAt, draftId (link na ActionDraft če autopilot), snapshotDate (link na BrainSnapshot če brain_digest), metadata (JSON string z dodatnimi podatki), createdAt. 4 indexi: type, severity, isRead, createdAt. (2) NEW `src/lib/notifications.ts` z 8 funkcijami: `createNotification(input)` — kreira zbirko z optional metadata (JSON.stringify); `getNotifications(query)` — vrača { notifications, stats: { total, unread, byType, bySeverity } } z filter options (type, severity, isRead, limit 1-200, days 1-365); `getUnreadCount()` — za bell badge (count preko vseh, no time window); `markAsRead(id)` — posodobi isRead + readAt; `markAllAsRead()` — vrača count updated; `deleteNotification(id)`; `deleteReadNotifications()` — cleanup prebranih; `cleanupOldNotifications(daysOld=90)` — daily cron briše starejše od 90 dni. Uporablja `getFreshDb()` pattern (kot v8.28-v8.31) za Turbopack-safe dostop do novih modelov. (3) Telegram integration — `src/lib/brain/telegram-notifications.ts`: `sendBrainDigest()` zdaj PRVORAČUNA masterBrain() (prej je bilo load-config-first), NATO vedno kliče `createNotification({ type:'brain_digest', severity: zdravje>=70?'success':zdravje>=50?'info':'warning', source:'brain', snapshotDate: današnji-datum, metadata: { healthScore, healthGrade, riskLevel, topActionCount, conflictsCount, projection30d/90d/12m } })` — Telegram send je še vedno isti (parseMode: null, plain text). `sendAutoPilotAlert(draft, reason)` — dodatno kliče `createNotification({ type:'autopilot_executed', severity:'success', source:'autopilot', draftId: draft.id, metadata: { uplift, confidence, domain, signal, rank, reason } })`. `sendAnomalyAlert(reason)` — dodatno kliče `createNotification({ type:'anomaly', severity:'error', source:'autopilot', metadata: { reason } })`. Vsi 3 kličejo createNotification v try/catch — če Telegram ni konfiguriran, je obvestilo VSE ENO zabeleženo. (4) NEW API `/api/brain-notifications` (NOT `/api/notifications` — obstoječi endpoint od v4.8 vrača Alert-based delivery history in ga uporablja notification-history-view.tsx + alerts-view.tsx, zato nov endpoint uporablja drugačno pot da ne zlomimo obstoječe funkcionalnosti). GET z query params (type/severity/isRead/limit/days) vrača { ok, notifications, stats }. POST z body (type, title, body, severity?, source?, draftId?, snapshotDate?, metadata?) — validation: type mora biti ena od 8 veljavnih, title in body required, severity mora biti ena od 4. PATCH z { action: 'mark_read'|'mark_all_read'|'delete_read', id? } za bulk akcije. (5) NEW API `/api/brain-notifications/[id]` — PATCH z { isRead: true } (samo mark-as-read je podprt), DELETE briše eno notifikacijo. (6) NEW API `/api/cron/cleanup-notifications` — GET + POST isti handler, daily cron ki kliče `cleanupOldNotifications(90)` in vrača { ok, deleted }. Auth: `?key=<MONITOR_CRON_KEY>` (dev mode no auth). (7) UI: (a) **Bell icon (🔔) v BrainSynthesisCard header** z unread count badge (red circle z številko, animate-pulse če >0, "99+" če >99). Click → dropdown z zadnjimi 5 neprebranimi notifications (vsaka: type icon + title + body truncated + time-ago + "✓ Preberi" gumb) + "Glej vse →" link ki scroll-a do full Notification Center section spodaj. Dropdown podpira Escape-to-close in click-outside-to-close. Polls `/api/brain-notifications?limit=5&days=7&isRead=false` vsakih 30s. (b) **Full Notification Center section** na dnu BrainSynthesisCard (after AccuracyTrendCard): orange/amber-tinted card z naslovom "🔔 Notification Center" + v8.38 badge + "N novo" pulse badge če unread > 0. Filter bar (3 dropdowns): type (Vsi tipi + 8 tipov z ikonami), severity (Vse teže + 4 možnosti z emoji), read filter (Vsa/Samo neprebrana/Samo prebrana). Stats row: "{total} skupaj · {unread} neprebranih · {count} {typeLabel} za vsak tip z vsaj 1 obvestilom". Scrollable list (max-h-96 overflow-y-auto) z notifikacijami: vsaka prikazuje type icon + title (z "neprebrano" pulse dot za neprebrane) + body truncated na 200 chars + severity pill (color-coded: info=sky, success=emerald, warning=amber, error=red) + time-ago (zdaj/X min nazaj/X h nazaj/X d nazaj/locale date) + source label + ✅ mark read button + 🗑️ delete button. Prikazujem zadnjih 30 dni. Tipi: 🧠 Brain digest · 🤖 Auto-pilot · ⚠️ Anomalija · 🔧 Sistem. Telegram + DB log — tudi če Telegram ni konfiguriran, so obvestila zabeležena tukaj." - **🧮 DEAL CALCULATOR + 📈 PROFIT TIMELINE CHART (POLISH PHASE — HITRA ODLOČITEV + VIZUALNO SLEDENJE).** Problem: (1) Uporabnik vidi oglas na Bolha (npr. iPhone 13 za 280€) ampak nima hitrega kalkulatorja ki bi povedal "če prodaš za 380€, profit je 85€ (30% ROI) po 15€ fee". (2) Dashboard nima časovnega grafa dobička — uporabnik ne vidi trenda (raste/pada/stabilen?). v8.37 dodaja: (1) **Deal Calculator** — `src/lib/trades/deal-calculator.ts` z `calculateDeal(input)` (pure math, no DB, no AI): totalCost = buyPrice + buyFees + shippingCost + refurbCost; totalRevenue = expectedSellPrice - sellFees; netProfit = totalRevenue - totalCost; roiPct = (netProfit/totalCost)×100; marginPct = (netProfit/totalRevenue)×100; breakEvenPrice = totalCost + sellFees; breakEvenMargin = expectedSellPrice - breakEvenPrice; dailyProfit = netProfit/avgHoldDays; weeklyProfit = dailyProfit×7; monthlyProfit = dailyProfit×30. Recommendation logic: BUY če ROI≥30% in profit≥30€ (zelena), MARGINAL če ROI≥15% in profit≥15€ (amber), PASS sicer (rdeča) + Slovenian human-readable reason. Risk assessment: LOW default, MEDIUM če hold>30 dni ali capital>500€ ali refurb>20% nabavne, HIGH če margin<15% ali cena pod break-even — 5 dejavnikov z opisi. API: GET `/api/ai/deal-calculator?buyPrice=280&expectedSellPrice=380&buyFees=0&sellFees=15` (vrača 85€ profit, 30.4% ROI, BUY) + POST `/api/ai/deal-calculator` z JSON body (accepts tudi optional shippingCost/refurbCost/avgHoldDays/category, accepts tudi , ali . decimal separator za Slovenian locale). UI: 🧮 Deal Calculator card na Dashboard z live calculation (klikaš vnose — rezultat se takoj posodobi, brez submit button-a), 6 input polj (buyPrice, expectedSellPrice, buyFees, sellFees, shippingCost opt, refurbCost opt) + hold days slider (1-60 dni, default 14), big profit number z barvno kodiranim ozadjem glede na recommendation (zelena BUY / amber MARGINAL / rdeča PASS), ROI% + margin% pod profitom, 3 mini-stats (break-even cena + marža, profit/dan, profit/teden + /mesec), recommendation pill z emoji (✅/⚠️/❌) + Slovenian reason text, risk level badge (🟢/🟡/🔴 LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH), risk factors list če obstajajo (bullet list z opisi), "💾 Shrani kot trade (held)" button ki POST-a v `/api/trades` z status='held' (user še ni kupil, samo analiza) in notes ki vsebuje vse deal calc projekcije (pričakovana cena, sellFees, ROI, marža, break-even, recommendation, hold dni) za poznejšo analizo in sledenje. (2) **Profit Timeline** — `src/lib/trades/profit-timeline.ts` z `getProfitTimeline(granularity='weekly', days=90)`: bere iz Trade tabele (status='sold', sellDate v zadnjih N dneh, sellPrice not null), grupira po ISO week (Monday start, label "T32 2026") ali calendar month (label "Avg 2026"), continuous timeline vključuje empty periods z 0 profit (da graf pokaže realne gap-e med prodajami), per-point: profit (sellPrice-sellFees-buyPrice-buyFees), revenue, cost, tradeCount, cumulativeProfit (running total). Po agregaciji: totalProfit, totalRevenue, totalCost, totalTrades, bestWeek/worstWeek (po profit, samo non-empty periods), avgWeeklyProfit (samo čez non-empty), trend (compare first half avg vs second half avg: GROWING če second > first × 1.1, DECLINING če < 0.9, STABLE če znotraj ±10%, INSUFFICIENT_DATA če <4 točke ali totalProfit=0). API: GET `/api/analytics/profit-timeline?granularity=weekly&days=90` z 5-min in-memory cache per (granularity, days) tuple da nehamo hammer-ati DB na dashboard refresh. UI: 📈 Profit Timeline card na Dashboard z recharts ComposedChart (Bar za weekly profit z zeleno/rdečo barvo glede na znak + Line za cumulative profit v amber), X-axis labels (T32 2026 / Avg 2026), Y-axis EUR format, custom tooltip z datum + profit + tradeCount + cumulative, ReferenceLine pri y=0, granularity toggle (Tedensko/Mesečno), days selector (30d/90d/12m), trend badge (↗️ RASTE / → STABILEN / ↘️ PADA / ❓ PREMAJHNO), 4 summary stats (skupaj profit, prodaj count, avg/teden ali /mesec, prihodki), best/worst period badge z label + profit, empty state "Ni dovolj prodaj za graf." Stats: AI endpointi 428→429 (+1: ai/deal-calculator; profit-timeline je pod /api/analytics/ ne /api/ai/), Total API routes 608→610 (+2: ai/deal-calculator + analytics/profit-timeline), Brain category 24 (nespremenjeno — noben brain file dodan), Analytics 72→73 (+1), funkcije ~295→~297 (+2: calculateDeal + getProfitTimeline). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. **Polish phase continues — hitra odločitev (Deal Calculator) + vizualno sledenje (Profit Timeline). v8.32 = monitoring, v8.33 = performance, v8.34 = testing, v8.35 = data+notifications, v8.36 = trade management, v8.37 = decision support + visualization.**

**v8.36 — 📊 Trade Management Enhancement (Polish phase — CSV Import + Quick Add + Dashboard Stats):**
- **📊 TRADE MANAGEMENT ENHANCEMENT (POLISH PHASE — CSV IMPORT + QUICK ADD + DASHBOARD STATS).** Problem: uporabnik je mogel dodajati trade-e samo ročno enega po enega (TradeFormDialog z 14 polji).
