Na Dashboard-u ni bilo hitrega "Dodaj trade" gumba (moral si navigirati v Skladišče → Nov trade). Dashboard ni imel kompaktnega trade statistike card-a z win rate + best niche (SkladisceWidget je podroben ampak dolg). v8.36 rešuje vse to in naredi sistem bolj uporaben za real-world usage. (1) **CSV Import** — `src/lib/trades/csv-import.ts` z `parseCsv()` (ročno napisan CSV parser: quoted fields z vejicami, escaped quotes, auto-detekcija `,` ali `;` ločila glede na prvo vrstico, BOM strip, `\r\n`/`\n` line endings — brez odvisnosti od papaparse/csv-parse) + `validateCsvRows()` (header mapping za slovenske `naslov`/`kategorija`/`nakupcena`/`nakupdatum`/... in angleške `title`/`category`/`buyPrice`/`buyDate`/..., obvezne kolone `title`+`buyPrice`, validacija cene/datuma/statusa, default-i za manjkajoče colonne) + `generateCsvTemplate()` (header + example vrstica). API: POST `/api/trades/import-csv` sprejme JSON `{csv: string}` ali multipart file upload (auto-detect po Content-Type), bulk `db.trade.createMany({data: [...]})` z enim Prisma klicem (performantno za 100+ trade-ov), vrne `{ok, created, errors, totalRows, validCount, errorCount}` — invalid rows se preskočijo in poročajo v `errors` array (partial success). GET `/api/trades/csv-template` vrne CSV predlogo z `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="trade-template.csv"`. (2) **Quick Add Trade Modal** — `src/components/dashboard/quick-add-trade-modal.tsx` reusable modal komponenta z 6 polji (title, category, status, buyPrice, buyLocation, sellPrice) namesto 14 v polnem TradeFormDialog. POST na `/api/trades`, auto-set status='sold' če je sellPrice podan. Profit+ROI preview, Enter-key submit, toast "✓ Trade dodan". Lahko odprt iz Dashboard, AI Hub, ali Trades view (props: `{open, onOpenChange, onSaved?}`). (3) **Dashboard Trade Stats card** — `src/components/dashboard/trade-stats-card.tsx` kompakten card z velikim profit number-jem (90d, barvno kodiran zeleno/amber/rdeče), povprečno maržo (%), 4 mini-stats (win rate % profitable sold trades, trade count sold·held, top niša z ROI%, slaba niša z ROI%) + best/worst trade na dnu. Fetcha 3 API-je vzparedno (Promise.all). "＋ Dodaj trade" button odpre QuickAddTradeModal. (5) **Trades-view modifications** — "📥 Uvozi CSV" button v action bar (zraven obstoječega CSV export button-a), CsvImportDialog z file upload + paste textarea + predogled (max 8 vrstic) + import result (3 stat box-e + list napak) + auto-close+refresh po 1.5s če ni napak, "📄 Prenesi predlogo CSV" link (direkten download). **v8.35 — 🌱 Seed Demo Data + 📱 Telegram Brain Notifications (Polish phase — "Make the system alive"):**
- **🌱 SEED DEMO DATA + 📱 TELEGRAM BRAIN NOTIFICATIONS (POLISH PHASE — "MAKE THE SYSTEM ALIVE").** Problem: sistem je tehnično popoln ampak PRAZEN — 0 trades v DB (Actual Profit 0€, accuracy nemogoče izračunati, vsi Brain signali uporabljajo default inputs); Brain priporočila NISO povezana z Telegram botom (uporabnik mora ročno preverjati dashboard). v8.35 dodaja: (1) **Seed Demo Data** — `src/lib/seed/demo-data.ts` z 25 realističnimi slovenskimi trade-i (4 izvori: Bolha/Vinted/Avtonet/mobile.de × 5 kategorij: elektronika/obutev/oblačila/avto/orodje × zadnjih 90 dni × mixed margins vključno z 1 namernim loss case (Adidas Yeezy 350: 120€ kupljeno, 90€ prodano = -39€ neto). 19 sold + 5 held (inventory aging signal) + 1 cancelled. Idempotent — `seedDemoData()` preveri `db.trade.count()` in skip-a če > 0; ne briše obstoječih. Po seeding: Actual Profit 90d = 973€ (non-zero!), best trade Alu platišča +145€, worst trade Yeezy -39€. API: GET `/api/ai/brain/seed` (count + byStatus) + POST `{action:'seed'|'clear'|'reseed'}`. UI: 🌱 Seed button (viden samo če count=0) na novo `SeedAndTelegramCard` komponento (lime+cyan gradient) — po seed-anju se stran samodejno reload-a da vsi brain-i poberejo nove podatke. (2) **Telegram Brain Notifications** — `src/lib/brain/telegram-notifications.ts` z 3 tipi: (a) **Daily Digest** cron @ 08:00 (`/api/cron/brain-digest`) pošlje TOP 5 akcij + overallHealth score/grade/riskLevel + strategy projections (30d/90d/12m profitEUR) + oneLineSummary + conflicts count; (b) **Auto-pilot Alert** — ko `runSafeAutoPilot()` izvede draft, kliče `sendAutoPilotAlert(draft, reason)` ki pošlje action/domain/signal/uplift/confidence/reason; (c) **Anomaly Alert** — ko je auto-pilot suspendiran (pre-existing ali mid-run preko `incrementHourlyCounter()`), kliče `sendAnomalyAlert(reason)`. Vsa 3 obvestila so NON-CRITICAL — `runSafeAutoPilot()` jih wrap-a v try/catch; če Telegram ni konfiguriran ali send faila, auto-pilot še vedno dela pravilno. Messages so PLAIN TEXT (`parseMode: null` v `sendTelegramMessage`) — nobenega MarkdownV2 escape-a potrebnega (EUR amounts, dates, parens delajo literalno). MODIFIED `src/lib/telegram.ts`: `SendMessageOptions.parseMode` optional parameter (default `MarkdownV2` za backward compat; `null` = plain text). API test: POST `/api/ai/brain/telegram-test` `{type:'digest'|'autopilot'|'anomaly'}` — autopilot/anomaly uporabljata mock draft; digest pošlje real Master Brain output. UI: 3 test gumbi na `SeedAndTelegramCard` z rezultatom (✅ Poslano / ❌ Telegram ni konfiguriran). Stats: AI endpointi 426→428 (+2: brain/seed + brain/telegram-test; brain-digest cron je pod /api/cron/), Total API routes 603→606 (+3: brain/seed + brain/telegram-test + cron/brain-digest), Brain category 22→24. Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. **Polish phase — making system alive with real data + Telegram integration. v8.32 = monitoring (System Health), v8.33 = performance (Caching + Stats), v8.34 = testing (Brain Integration Tests), v8.35 = data + notifications (Seed Demo Data + Telegram Brain Notifications).**

**v8.34 — 🧪 Brain Integration Test Suite (Polish phase continues — "Does the Brain system actually work?"):**
- **🧪 BRAIN INTEGRATION TEST SUITE (POLISH PHASE CONTINUES).** Problem: 8 brain layers + auto-pilot + draft queue + adaptive weights — ampak samo 7 obstoječih testov (none for Brain system). We're shipping untested code. v8.34 dodaja 92 novih integration testov v `tests/brain/` (8 novih testnih datotek): (1) **7 domain brain tests** (`domain-brains.test.ts`) — profit/inventory/market/sourcing/risk/buyer/pricing vsak: default inputs valid, custom inputs accepted, 6 signals z valid grades (A+/A/B/C/D/F, score 0-100). (2) **Master Brain orchestration test** (`master-brain.test.ts`) — 9 testov: orchestrates all 7 domain brains (domainSummary.length=7, topActions.length=5), conflicts array, overallHealth score 0-100 z LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL riskLevel, strategy projections (30d/90d/12m profitEUR > 0), oneLineSummary (>20 chars), skip flags (skipProfit → domains.profit=null, domainSummary.length=6), all 7 raw brain results exposed, bottlenecks+strengths arrays, topActions ranked 1..5 z monotonically decreasing finalScore. (3) **Explainability test** (`explainability.test.ts`) — 6 testov: generates 5 explanations z reasoning (>20 chars) + reasoningParts (trigger/whyRankedHere/expectedOutcome) + trustScore 0-100 per action + overall trustScore + summaryBlurb; balanced profile → null profileImpact; conservative profile → non-null profileImpact on filtered actions. (4) **Scenario Brain test** (`scenario.test.ts`) — 8 testov: 3 preset scenarios returned, comparisonTable ≥6 rows, aggressive > conservative 12m projection, custom overrides supported, preset configs (capitalMultiplier 0.7/1.0/1.5), 3 scenarios per metric row, full MasterBrainResult attached, recommendation reasoning references bestScenario. (5) **Risk Profile test** (`risk-profile.test.ts`) — 10 testov: balanced → no adjustment, conservative → REDUCE_RISK (health<70), aggressive → ACCEPT_RISK (health>40), conservative 0.5x risk budget, aggressive 1.5x risk budget, validateProfile accepts valid + rejects invalid (bad tolerance, max>100, negative liquidity, bad horizon), DEFAULT_PROFILE is balanced. (6) **Adaptive Weights test** (`adaptive-weights.test.ts`) — 11 testov: boost when executionRate>0.8, reduce when <0.4, no change 0.4-0.8, no change when 0 actions, clamp [0.5, 2.0] upper+lower, reason always non-empty, DEFAULT_DOMAIN_WEIGHTS has 7 domains, ADAPTIVE_WEIGHTS_CONSTANTS exposes thresholds, risk highest (1.3), buyer lowest (0.9). (7) **Auto-pilot eligibility test** (`auto-pilot.test.ts`) — 16 testov: V1 (8 safety rules: disabled, conservative, HIGH, uplift≥100€, domain=risk, daily limit, daily budget, eligible pass) + V2 (6 tests: aggressive allows MEDIUM, rejects HIGH, rejects uplift≥300€, daily limit up to 10, rejects domain=risk both modes, returns 8 audit reasons) + Config defaults (DEFAULT_AUTOPILOT_CONFIG safe defaults OFF/safe/5/500, AGGRESSIVE_CONFIG higher thresholds 10/2000/300/anomaly=8). (8) **Cache stats test** (`cache-stats.test.ts`) — 11 testov: tracks hits+misses+hitRate=50, tracks sets, getAllCacheStats returns all namespaces, resetCacheStats clears specific+all, getCacheStats 0 for unknown namespace, hitRate 100 when only hits, hitRate 0 when only misses, getCacheStoreSize, cached value round-trips, expired entries count as miss. Test count: 37 → 129 (92 new). AI endpointi: 426 (unchanged — tests only). Total API routes: 603 (unchanged). Lint: 0 napak ✨. Typecheck: 0 napak ✨. All 129 tests passing ✅. **Polish phase continues. v8.32 = monitoring (System Health), v8.33 = performance (Caching + Stats), v8.34 = testing (Brain Integration Test Suite). **v8.33 — ⚡ Performance Caching + Cache Stats (Polish phase continues — "How fast is the Brain system? Is the cache working?"):**
- **⚡ PERFORMANCE CACHING + CACHE STATS (POLISH PHASE CONTINUES).** Problem: Master Brain kliče 7 domain brain-ov preko `Promise.all`, ampak ni bilo vidnosti v cache hit rates in response times. Uporabnik ne more videti na en pogled: "Ali cache dejansko dela? Koliko hit-ov imamo? Kateri brain je najpočasnejši?" v8.33 dodaja: (1) **Cache stats tracking v `ai-cache.ts`** — `getCachedAIWithStats(namespace, key)`, `setCachedAIWithStats(namespace, key, value, ttlMs)`, `getCacheStats(namespace)`, `getAllCacheStats()`, `resetCacheStats(namespace?)`, `getCacheStoreSize()` — hit/miss/sets counters per namespace (master-brain, profit-brain, inventory-brain, market-brain, sourcing-brain, risk-brain, buyer-brain, pricing-brain). Memory-only Map, process restart briše. Backward compat preserved: actual stored key je še vedno `${namespace}:${key}` — samo buildCacheKey v vsakem route-u vrne suffix, namespace prefix pa doda stats wrapper. (2) **Performance metrics module (`src/lib/brain/performance.ts`)** — response time tracking z rolling window (zadnjih 100 vstopov per brain), computes avg/p50/p95/p99/min/max + `cacheHitRate` (derived from per-call `cached` flag). Memory-only Map, process restart briše. (3) **`withPerf(brain, fn, cached)` wrapper** za easy instrumentation — wrap-a async funkcijo in avtomatsko record-a duration + cached flag. Also export-a `recordPerf(brain, durationMs, cached)` za manual recording (npr. cache-hit path kjer funkcija ni klicana). `getPerfStats(brain)`, `getAllPerfStats()`, `resetPerfStats(brain?)` za API/UI consumption. (4) **Performance API endpoint (`src/app/api/ai/brain/performance/route.ts`)** — GET vrača `cacheStats` (per-namespace) + `perfStats` (per-brain) + `cacheStoreSize` + `summary` (overallHitRate weighted, totalRequests, totalCached, avgResponseTimeMs = mean of all brain avgMs, p95ResponseTimeMs = max p95 across all brains) + `source: 'v8.33-performance'`. POST `{ action: 'reset' }` clear-a vse counters (NE clear-a underlying cache store — samo counters). runtime='nodejs', dynamic='force-dynamic', maxDuration=30. **v8.31 — 🚀 Aggressive Auto-pilot + Anomaly Detection (🎯 AUTOMATION PHASE COMPLETE):**
- **🚀 AGGRESSIVE AUTO-PILOT + ANOMALY DETECTION (🎯 AUTOMATION PHASE COMPLETE).** v8.30 je dodal Safe Auto-pilot (LOW risk only, 8 safety rules, rollback capability). v8.31 zaključi Automation phase z: (1) **Aggressive mode** (opt-in, double confirmation) — dovoli tudi MEDIUM confidence (ne le LOW), višji limiti (10/dan, 2000€/dan), uplift < 300€. HIGH confidence je še vedno vedno manual (oba modal — tudi v aggressive). Domain='risk' je še vedno vedno izključen (oba modal — risk mitigation zahteva human judgment). (2) **Anomaly detection** — če auto-pilot izvede >8 akcij v 1 uri (default `anomalyHourlyThreshold=8` iz `AGGRESSIVE_CONFIG`), se samodejno suspendira (possible loop), zahteva manual re-enable preko POST `{action:'clear_anomaly'}`. (3) **Double opt-in za aggressive mode** — prvi klik nastavi pending confirmation (5-min window preko `AGGRESSIVE_CONFIRM_WINDOW_MS=5*60*1000`), drugi klik znotraj 5 minut potrdi in aktivira aggressive mode (set-a `autoPilotMode='aggressive'`, clear-a `autoPilotAggressiveConfirmedAt`). Po 5 minutah potrditev poteče — moraš ponovno. (4) **UI izboljšave** — mode selector z double-click confirmation (Safe button aktivna ko mode='safe'; Aggressive button prikaže "✅ Potrdi Aggressive" ko čaka potrditev, "🚀 Aggressive (MEDIUM OK)" ko active), anomaly banner (red, eye-catching — top of card, z "Razveljavi suspenzijo" button), threshold comparison box (Safe vs Aggressive side-by-side, 6 pravil vsak), enhanced stats z mode-aware prikazom ("Danes: 3/10 akcij (aggressive) · 145€/2000€ budget"), hourly counter za anomaly awareness ("Zadnja ura: 2 akciji" z warning če ≥6). **v8.28 — 🎛️ Adaptive Domain Weights (Intelligence phase continues — FEEDBACK LOOP, "Master Brain se uči iz tvojega vedenja"):**
- **🎛️ ADAPTIVE DOMAIN WEIGHTS (FEEDBACK LOOP).** Problem: Master Brain (v8.22) uporablja HARDCODED domain weights (risk=1.3, profit=1.2, sourcing=1.1, ...). Ampak vsak uporabnik je drugačen — nekateri prioritizirajo profit, drugi risk. Stated preference (kar uporabnik reče) se pogosto razlikuje od revealed preference (kar uporabnik dejansko stori). v8.28 naredi uteži ADAPTIVE — shranjene v Settings tabeli (JSON `adaptiveDomainWeights` polje), Master Brain jih naloži namesto hardcoded. Feedback loop: ko uporabnik označi akcijo kot 'executed' ali 'rejected', sistem beleži per-domain execution stats. Po vsakih 10 akcijah na domeno: executionRate > 0.8 → weight × 1.1 (boost — user values this domain), < 0.4 → × 0.9 (reduce — user ignores this domain), clamp [0.5, 2.0]. NOVO: `prisma/schema.prisma` (Settings model + `adaptiveDomainWeights String?` field). **v8.22 — 🧠✨ Master Brain (FINAL Brain layer — APEX hierarchy, orkestrira vseh 7 Domain Brain-ov v ENO končno odločitev):**
- **Master Brain** — VRHUNEC Brain arhitekture. Pokliče vseh 7 Domain Brain funkcij VZPARALELNO preko direct TS imports (`Promise.all([profitBrain(), inventoryBrain(), marketBrain(), sourcingBrain(), riskBrain(), buyerBrain(), pricingBrain()])`) — NE preko HTTP, tako ni fan-out overhead. Zbere 21+ akcij (3 per domeno × 7 domen), jih ranked-a TOP 5 po `expectedUpliftEUR × confidenceWeight × domainWeight` (confidenceWeight: HIGH=1.0, MEDIUM=0.7, LOW=0.4; domainWeight: risk=1.3, profit=1.2, pricing=1.1, sourcing=1.1, inventory=1.0, market=1.0, buyer=0.9). Zazna 5 TIPOV KONFLIKTOV med domeni: (1) Profit vs Risk (Profit želi več voluma, a Risk opozarja na koncentracijo — HIGH severity), (2) Market vs Inventory (trg pada MARKDOWN faza, inventar zastaral — HIGH), (3) Sourcing vs Pricing (best source margin <20% in pricing margin score <50 — MEDIUM), (4) Buyer vs Risk (churn >15% in fraud score <50 — MEDIUM), (5) Pricing vs Market (Pricing predlaga višje cene, trg BEARISH — MEDIUM). Izračuna overallHealth score (0-100 weighted: profit 0.20, risk 0.20, inventory 0.15, market 0.15, sourcing 0.10, buyer 0.10, pricing 0.10), grade (A+/A/B/C/D/F), riskLevel (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL), bottlenecks (D/F domene) in strengths (A+/A domene). Generira 30d/90d/12m strategijo (30d/90d = sinthesized EUR-profit projekcija iz profit + sourcing + pricing + buyer domen; 12m = 90d × 4 × 0.9 z 10% uncertainty discount). Vrne ONE oneLineSummary "Danes: <top akcija>. 30d: <profitEUR>€. Tveganje: <riskScore>/100. Zdravje: <grade>." ki odgovarja na vprašanje "Kaj naj naredim danes?". Pure deterministic compute (aiUsed: false, no AI/LLM SDK). 10-min cache (longer ker 5-min individual Domain Brain cache ker agregira 7 Brain-ov). `src/lib/brain/master.ts` (pure compute, async, imports vseh 7 Domain Brain funkcij, NO next/server, NO Prisma). `src/app/api/ai/brain/master/route.ts` (GET+POST, maxDuration=60, force-dynamic, runtime=nodejs, 10-min cache, no direct DB injection — posamezne Domain Brain-i sami injicirajo DB state preko svojih route-ov). AI Hub: BrainSynthesisCard dobi Master Brain BANNER na vrhu (gold/amber gradient, Crown icon, 🧠✨ MASTER BRAIN z v8.22 badge + FINAL · APEX badge) — sedaj prikazuje ONE prominent banner + 7 stacked domain sectionov pod njim. **🎯 FINAL MILESTONE: v8.22 zaključuje celotno Brain arhitekturo. 7 Domain Brains (42 signalov) + 1 Master Brain = hierarhija specialist → domain → master. Maksimalna inteligenca 414 specialistov, organizirana v eno zavestno odločitev.**

**v8.14 — AI Profit Density Maximizer & AI Deal Source Profit Compounding Maximizer & AI Inventory Working Capital Maximizer (3 funkcije — PROFIT DENSITY per ura/per kategorija & COMPOUNDING annual profit growth čez deal sources z source prioritization & WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY med fast in slow movers focus):**

- **AI Profit Density Maximizer** — AI MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT DENSITY — profit pridobljen na enoto aktivnosti (per listing, per kategorijo, per uro vlaganja v sourcing). Višja gostota = več profit-a z manj truda. "Tvoj profit density je 12€/h. Z fokuso na high-density niches lahko dosežeš 14€/h (×1.18)." Razlika od profit-per-cycle-maximizer (v8.12 ki maksimizira profit per cycle €/cycle z maximizationLevers in cycleVsVolumeTradeoff) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT DENSITY (profit per ura + per kategorija + per listing, ne per-cycle €). Razlika od profit-per-trade-scaling-maximizer (v8.13 ki skalira profit per trade skozi 4-fazno pot CURRENT→OPTIMIZED→PREMIUM→ELITE) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT DENSITY (profit per unit aktivnosti, ne per-trade € scaling). Razlika od deal-source-profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.14 ki maksimizira compounding profit growth čez deal sources z reinvestment prioritizacijo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT DENSITY (profit per ura/per kategorijo snapshot, ne compounding annual profit growth). Razlika od inventory-working-capital-maximizer (v8.14 ki maksimizira working capital efficiency med fast in slow movers) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT DENSITY (profit per unit aktivnosti, ne capital turnover × margin). Razlika od profit-per-euro-maximizer (v8.07 ki maksimizira profit per € deployed) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT DENSITY (profit per ura + per kategorijo + per listing, ne € profit / € capital). current { profitPerHour €/h [0, 10000] (= avgProfitPerTrade / hoursInvestedPerTrade), profitPerCategory € [0, 100000] (= (avgProfitPerTrade × tradesPerMonth) / categoryCount), profitPerListing € [0, 10000] (= avgProfitPerTrade — one trade ~ one listing), densityScore [0, 100] (= clamp((profitPerHour × 0.5 + profitPerCategory × 0.3 + avgProfitPerTrade × 0.2) / 10, 0, 100)) }. maximization { maximizedDensity [0, 100] (= densityScore × 1.18 — typical density uplift ko fokusiraš na high-density niches), maximizationFactor 1.18 (constant uplift), densityGrade A/B/C/D (A če ≥ 70, B ≥ 50, C ≥ 30, else D), optimalStrategy FOCUS_HIGH_DENSITY_NICHES | REDUCE_LOW_DENSITY_TRADING | MAINTAIN_CURRENT_MIX (heuristic: high score → maintain mix, medium → focus high-density, low → reduce low-density), focusNiches ["electronics", "sneakers", "tools"] (3 high-density niches za fokus) }. Deterministic formula-based maximizer (no AI call, no DB query). GET+POST (handleProfitDensityMaximizer shared function — AI Hub runner kompatibilnost). Inputs iz query string ali POST body z sensible defaults (avgProfitPerTrade 25€, tradesPerMonth 8, hoursInvestedPerTrade 2.5h, categoryCount 3).
- **AI Deal Source Profit Compounding Maximizer** — AI MAKSIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING profit growth čez deal sources — reinvestira profit iz enega source-a v višji-yield source-ih. Compound growth rate maximizer. "Tvoj compounding rate je 30€/mo/source. Z prioritizacijo high-yield source-ov lahko dosežeš 4140€ annual compounded (×1.15)." Razlika od profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira compounding reinvest rate na nivoju portfolio) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING čez DEAL SOURCES (per-source compounding + source prioritization, ne portfolio-level reinvest rate). Razlika od profit-density-maximizer (v8.14 ki maksimizira profit density per ura/per kategorijo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING annual profit growth čez sources (ne profit per unit aktivnosti snapshot). Razlika od inventory-working-capital-maximizer (v8.14 ki maksimizira working capital efficiency med fast in slow movers) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING profit growth (reinvestment × source count × growth rate, ne capital turnover × margin). Razlika od capital-growth-maximizer (ki maksimizira capital growth z reinvestment rate) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING PROFIT čez deal sources (source prioritization + exponential growth curve, ne generic capital growth). Razlika od profit-growth-rate-maximizer (v8.11 ki maksimizira growth rate skupnega profit-a v %/mo MoM) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING annual profit z reinvestRate × sourceCount × growthRate exponent (compounding math, ne linear MoM growth). current { monthlyCompoundingRate €/mo per source [0, 100000] (= (avgMonthlyProfit × reinvestRate) / sourceCount), compoundedAnnualProfit € [0, 10000000] (= avgMonthlyProfit × 12 × (1 + (growth/100) × reinvestRate)^sourceCount), compoundingScore [0, 100] (= clamp((compoundedAnnualProfit / 5000) × 100, 0, 100)), reinvestRate [0, 1] }. maximization { maximizedCompoundedProfit € [0, 10000000] (= compoundedAnnualProfit × 1.15 — source prioritization optimization), sourcePrioritization 1.15 (constant uplift), compoundingGrade A/B/C/D (A če ≥ 70, B ≥ 50, C ≥ 30, else D), optimalStrategy PRIORITIZE_HIGH_YIELD_SOURCES | INCREASE_REINVESTMENT | DIVERSIFY_SOURCES (heuristic: low reinvest → increase, few sources → diversify, else prioritize), projectedGrowthCurve "EXPONENTIAL" }. Deterministic formula-based maximizer (no AI call, no DB query). GET+POST (handleDealSourceProfitCompoundingMaximizer shared function — AI Hub runner kompatibilnost). Inputs iz query string ali POST body z sensible defaults (avgMonthlyProfit 200€, reinvestRate 0.6, sourceCount 4, avgProfitGrowthRate 5%).
- **AI Inventory Working Capital Maximizer** — AI MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY — minimizira kapital vezan v slow-moving inventory, maksimizira kapital v fast-movers. "Tvoj working capital turnover je 32×/leto z 28% weighted margin. Capital efficiency score 18/100. Z shiftanjem 70% kapitala v fast movers lahko dosežeš 22/100 (×1.22)." Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per item z reallocation) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY (turnover × margin combined score, ne per-item capital efficiency). Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-growth-maximizer (v8.12 ki maksimizira capital efficiency growth %/mo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY snapshot (turnover × margin × fast/slow mix, ne %/mo growth rate). Razlika od inventory-cash-conversion-maximizer (ki maksimizira cash conversion cycle) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY (turnover × margin × fast/slow mix, ne cash conversion cycle days). Razlika od profit-density-maximizer (v8.14 ki maksimizira profit density per ura/per kategorijo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY (turnover × weighted margin, ne profit per unit aktivnosti). Razlika od deal-source-profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.14 ki maksimizira compounding profit growth čez deal sources) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY med fast in slow movers (capital turnover × margin, ne compounding annual profit growth). Razlika od inventory-annual-yield-maximizer (v8.11 ki maksimizira annual yield inventory-ja) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA WORKING CAPITAL EFFICIENCY (turnover × weighted margin z fast/slow mix, ne letni yield %). current { workingCapitalTurnover ×/year [0, 500] (= fastMoverPct × (365/daysFast) + slowMoverPct × (365/daysSlow)), weightedProfitMargin % [0, 100] (= fastMoverPct × marginFast + slowMoverPct × marginSlow), capitalEfficiencyScore [0, 100] (= clamp((turnover × margin) / 50, 0, 100)), fastMoverCapitalPct [0, 1] }. maximization { maximizedCapitalEfficiency [0, 100] (= score × 1.22 — shift more capital to fast movers), maximizationFactor 1.22 (constant uplift), capitalGrade A/B/C/D (A če ≥ 70, B ≥ 50, C ≥ 30, else D), optimalStrategy SHIFT_CAPITAL_TO_FAST_MOVERS | LIQUIDATE_SLOW_MOVERS | BALANCE_PORTFOLIO (heuristic: low fast-mover share → shift, very low score → liquidate, high score → balance), recommendedFastMoverPct 0.7 (constant target). Deterministic formula-based maximizer (no AI call, no DB query). GET+POST (handleInventoryWorkingCapitalMaximizer shared function — AI Hub runner kompatibilnost). Inputs iz query string ali POST body z sensible defaults (capitalDeployed 1500€, fastMoverCapitalPct 0.55, avgDaysToSellFast 7, avgDaysToSellSlow 45, avgProfitMarginFast 25%, avgProfitMarginSlow 35%).

**v8.13 — AI Profit Per Trade Scaling Maximizer & AI Deal Source Volume Growth Maximizer & AI Inventory Turnover Profit Growth Maximizer (3 funkcije — PER-TRADE 4-PHASE SCALING €/trade & PER-SOURCE VOLUME GROWTH RATE %/mo & TURNOVER PROFIT GROWTH RATE %/mo focus):**
- **AI Profit Per Trade Scaling Maximizer** — AI MAKSIMIZIRA in SKALIRA PROFIT PER TRADE — ne samo optimizira trenutni profit per trade, ampak ga sistematično SKALIRA GOR skozi 4 faze (CURRENT → OPTIMIZED → PREMIUM → ELITE). "Tvoj profit per trade je 45€. Za skaliranje na 100€/trade rabiš: premium sourcing, professional photos in cross-platform premium pricing." Razlika od profit-per-day-scaling-maximizer (v8.08 ki skalira daily profit z requiredTradesPerDay in requiredCapital per phase) — ta SKALIRA PROFIT PER TRADE €/trade (per-trade scaling, ne daily profit scaling). Razlika od deal-source-profit-margin-growth-maximizer (v8.12 ki maksimizira margin growth rate per source v %/mo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA in SKALIRA PROFIT PER TRADE v absolutnem €/trade (absolute per-trade scaling, ne per-source %/mo margin growth). Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-growth-maximizer (v8.12 ki maksimizira capital efficiency growth %/mo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING (€/trade scaling path, ne capital efficiency %/mo growth). Razlika od profit-per-trade-growth-maximizer (v8.10 ki maksimizira growth rate profit-a PER TRADE v €/mo) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA in SKALIRA profit per trade z scalingPath in scalingActions (phase-based scaling, ne growth rate). Razlika od profit-growth-rate-maximizer (v8.11 ki maksimizira growth rate skupnega profit-a v %/mo MoM) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING (per-trade € scaling, ne skupni profit %/mo growth). Razlika od profit-multiplier-maximizer (v8.09 ki maksimizira max profit multiplier z 6 dimensions) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA in SKALIRA PROFIT PER TRADE z 4-phase progression in scalingBottlenecks. Razlika od profit-per-euro-maximizer (v8.07 ki maksimizira profit per € deployed) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING (€/trade scaling, ne € profit per € capital). Razlika od inventory-annual-yield-maximizer (v8.11 ki maksimizira annual yield inventory-ja) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING (per-trade scaling, ne letni yield %). Razlika od deal-source-profit-per-day-maximizer (v8.11 ki maksimizira profit per day per source €/dan) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA in SKALIRA PROFIT PER TRADE (€/trade scaling path, ne €/dan per source). Razlika od profit-per-cycle-maximizer (v8.12 ki maksimizira profit per cycle z optimalCycleStrategy HIGH_MARGIN_LOW_VOLUME) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING z 4-phase scalingPath in 6 scalingActions (PREMIUM_SOURCING/PROFESSIONAL_PHOTOS/CROSS_PLATFORM_PREMIUM/BUNDLE_UPSELL/CERTIFICATION/TIMING_OPTIMIZATION). Razlika od inventory-turnover-profit-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira profit preko optimal inventory turnover) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING (per-trade € scaling, ne turnover-profit curve). Razlika od revenue-per-trade-maximizer (v8.06 ki maksimizira top-line sell price per trade) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA NET PROFIT PER TRADE SCALING (net €/trade scaling path, ne top-line revenue/trade). "Current: 45.00€/trade (50 SOLD 12m, total 2250€, trend 2.50%/mo, best 120€/worst 5€). Scaling: 180.00€/trade ELITE (×4.00 multiplier, grade B). Path: CURRENT→OPTIMIZED→PREMIUM→ELITE. 6 actions (PREMIUM_SOURCING +30%, TIMING_OPTIMIZATION +45%, PROFESSIONAL_PHOTOS +25%)." current { avgProfitPerTrade €/trade [0, 10000] (= avg((sellPrice − sellFees) − (buyPrice + buyFees)) over SOLD 12m), soldCount12m, totalProfit12m €, bestTrade € (max single-trade profit), worstTrade € (min single-trade profit), avgSellPrice €, avgBuyCost € (= avg(buyPrice + buyFees)), profitPerTradeTrend %/mo (linear regression slope / mean × 100 over monthly avg profit/trade), avgProfitPerTradeGrowthRate %/mo (echoes profitPerTradeTrend — kept for AI clarity), bestTradeRatio [0, 100] (bestTrade / avgProfitPerTrade — how much upside exists) }. maximization { scalingPath 4 { phase CURRENT/OPTIMIZED/PREMIUM/ELITE, targetProfitPerTrade € [0, 10000] (CURRENT = current × 1.0, OPTIMIZED = current × 1.5, PREMIUM = current × 2.5, ELITE = current × 4.0 capped at min(current × 5, 10000) — anti-hallucination), requirements (slovenski, max 300 — kaj zahteva ta faza), timeline (slovenski, max 200 — kdaj dosegljivo), feasibility [0, 100] (CURRENT 100, OPTIMIZED 80, PREMIUM 60, ELITE 40) }, maximizedProfitPerTrade €/trade [0, 10000] (= ELITE phase target — optimal achievable), scalingMultiplier [1.0, 5.0] (= maximized / current — anti-hallucination cap at 5.0), scalingActions 6 { action PREMIUM_SOURCING/PROFESSIONAL_PHOTOS/CROSS_PLATFORM_PREMIUM/BUNDLE_UPSELL/CERTIFICATION/TIMING_OPTIMIZATION (relative uplift 30/25/20/15/10/45%), expectedLift % [0, 100], difficulty LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH }, scalingBottlenecks 3-5 (slovenski, max 200 vsak — kaj limitira scaling profit per trade), scalingProjection 3 { months 3/6/12, projectedProfitPerTrade € [0, 10000] (linear ramp: 3m=25%, 6m=50%, 12m=100% adoption) }, scalingGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (A+ če maximized ≥ 500, A ≥ 300, B ≥ 150, C ≥ 75, D ≥ 25, else F) }. Anti-hallucination: profits [0, 10000], multiplier [1.0, 5.0]. 6h cache (key `profit-per-trade-scaling-maximizer:${currentMonth}`). Deterministic fallback (4-phase scaling path × 6 scalingActions × 3-5 bottlenecks + 3/6/12 month projection + grade + multiplier × 5.0 cap). GET+POST (handleProfitPerTradeScalingMaximizer shared function — AI Hub runner kompatibilnost). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trgovin → grade F.

Per source: profitPerWeek €/teden [0, 10000] (= total 12m profit / 52), tradesPerWeek [0, 500] (= tradeCount / 52), avgProfitPerTrade € [-5000, 50000], profitVelocityTrend % [-100, 200] (slope of weekly profit over 4 quarters), velocityScore [0, 100] (magnitude × trend × frequency combined). maximization per source { velocityMaximizationAction INCREASE_FREQUENCY/INCREASE_PROFIT_PER_TRADE/EXPAND_CATEGORIES/ADD_MONITORS/OPTIMIZE_TIMING (multiplier 1.40/1.25/1.30/1.35/1.18), maximizedProfitPerWeek €/teden [0, 10000] (≥ current, ≤ current × 1.5 ali +2000€), velocityUplift €/teden [0, 10000], velocityLevers 3-5 (max 200, slovenski — specific levers per source), frequencyScalingPlan (max 500, slovenski — kako varno povečati trades per week v 8 tednih), velocityProjection 3 { weeks 4/8/12, projectedProfitPerWeek €/teden [0, 10000] (linear ramp: 4w=33%, 8w=67%, 12w=100% adoption) } }. portfolio { totalCurrentVelocity €/teden, totalMaximizedVelocity €/teden, totalVelocityUplift €/teden, sourceVelocityRanking (rank by currentVelocity), bestVelocitySource }. Anti-hallucination: velocities [0, 10000], scores [0, 100]. 6h cache (key `deal-source-profit-velocity-maximizer:${currentMonth}`). GET+POST (handleDealSourceProfitVelocityMaximizer shared function — AI Hub runner kompatibilnost). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trgovin.
- **AI Inventory Return On Capital Maximizer** — AI MAKSIMIZIRA RETURN ON CAPITAL (ROC) za HELD inventory — ne samo ROI per item ampak overall ROC celotne inventory portfolio. "Tvoj ROC je 22%, ampak bi lahko bil 38% z boljšim inventory mix in hitrejšim turnover." Razlika od inventory-capital-return-maximizer (v8.07 ki maksimizira return OF capital — koliko deployed capital se vrne) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA RETURN ON CAPITAL (ROC = unrealized profit / total capital deployed × 100, ne return rate of capital). Razlika od inventory-annualized-return-maximizer (v8.06 ki maksimizira annualized % return na held inventory) — ta maksimizira ROC z rocMaximizationLevers in capitalReallocationPlan (ne sam % annualized return). Razlika od inventory-roi-maximizer-pro (v7.99 ki maksimizira ROI per item) — ta maksimizira PORTFOLIO ROC z optimalPortfolioComposition in rocVsBenchmark. Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per item z reallocation) — ta maksimizira ROC z inventoryMixOptimization (ne sam per-item efficiency). Razlika od inventory-cash-yield-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira annualized cash yield) — ta maksimizira ROC (return on capital, ne cash yield). Razlika od inventory-turnover-yield-maximizer (v8.05 ki maksimizira yield z yieldCurve) — ta maksimizira ROC z rocProjection (3/6/12 month). Razlika od inventory-yield-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira yield % per item) — ta maksimizira PORTFOLIO ROC z rocGrade in rocVsBenchmark. Razlika od profit-per-euro-maximizer (v8.07 ki maksimizira profit per euro deployed čez SOLD+HELD) — ta maksimizira ROC samo za HELD inventory (return ON capital %, ne €/€ ratio). Razlika od inventory-capital-allocator (v7.97 ki alloca capital per item) — ta maksimizira ROC z capitalReallocationPlan med low-ROC in high-ROC items. "Current: 22.00% ROC (5000€ deployed, 1100€ unrealized, 15 HELD). Maximized: 38.00% (uplift +16.00pp, grade B). Vs stocks: +30pp, vs bank: +36pp." current { totalCapitalDeployed € [0, 1000000] (= sum capital over HELD), totalUnrealizedProfit € [-500000, 1000000] (= sum estValue − capital over HELD), currentROC % [-50, 500] (= unrealizedProfit / totalCapitalDeployed × 100), annualizedROC % [-100, 1000] (= currentROC × 365 / avgHoldDays), heldInventoryCount, avgHoldDays [1, 730], avgEstValue € }. maximization { maximizedROC % [-50, 500] (≥ current, ≤ current × 1.8 ali +200pp absolute uplift), rocUplift pp [0, 300] (improvement = maximized − current), rocMaximizationLevers 4 { lever OPTIMIZE_INVENTORY_MIX/FASTER_TURNOVER/BETTER_SOURCING/REDUCE_IDLE_CAPITAL, potentialGain pp [0, 200] (OPTIMIZE_INVENTORY_MIX ~8, FASTER_TURNOVER ~6, BETTER_SOURCING ~5, REDUCE_IDLE_CAPITAL ~3), action (max 200, slovenski) }, inventoryMixOptimization { increaseCategories 3-5, decreaseCategories 3-5, rationale (max 400, slovenski) }, capitalReallocationPlan 3-5 { fromCategory, toCategory, amount € [0, 1000000], expectedRocGain pp [0, 200] }, rocProjection 3 { months 3/6/12, projectedROC % [-50, 500] (linear ramp: 3m=25%, 6m=50%, 12m=100% adoption), projectedProfit € [-1000000, 5000000] (= totalCapital × projectedROC / 100 × months/12) }, rocGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (A+ če maximized ≥ 100% ali uplift ≥ 50pp, A ≥ 50/30, B ≥ 25/15, C ≥ 10/5, D ≥ 0/1, else F), rocVsBenchmark { bankDeposit % = 2, stocks % = 8, realEstate % = 5, yourCurrentROC %, yourMaximizedROC %, maximizedVsBank pp, maximizedVsStocks pp, maximizedVsRealEstate pp }, optimalPortfolioComposition (max 500, slovenski — 40% high-ROC quick-flip, 35% premium slow-flip, 20% seasonal/bundle, 5% reserve) }. Anti-hallucination: ROC [-50, 500], scores [0, 100]. 6h cache (key `inventory-return-on-capital-maximizer:${currentMonth}:${heldItemIdsHash}` — invalidira ko held inventory se spremeni). Deterministic fallback (4 levers z potentialGain + mix optimization + reallocation plan med top/bottom 3 categories × 30% capital + 3/6/12m projection + benchmark comparison + grade + optimal composition). **v8.07 — AI Profit Per Euro Maximizer & AI Deal Source Revenue Maximizer & AI Inventory Capital Return Maximizer (3 funkcije — PROFIT PER EURO & REVENUE PER SOURCE & CAPITAL RETURN focus):**

- **AI Inventory Capital Return Maximizer** — AI MAKSIMIZIRA CAPITAL RETURN — koliko deployed kapitala se VRNE (return OF capital, ne return ON capital). Razlika od inventory-turnover-yield-maximizer (v8.05 ki maksimizira yield z yieldCurve in optimalTurnoverRate) — ta maksimizira ANNUALIZED RETURN (not yield curve) z optimalHoldTime in returnProjection. Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per item z reallocation) — ta maksimizira ANNUALIZED RETURN (realized + unrealized) z returnMaximizationLevers. Razlika od inventory-roi-maximizer-pro (v7.99 ki maksimizira ROI per item) — ta maksimizira ANNUALIZED ROI (per-item ROI × 365/holdDays) z benchmark comparison. Razlika od inventory-profit-per-day-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira daily profit per item) — ta maksimizira annualized % return (compared with stocks/bonds). Razlika od inventory-cash-conversion-maximizer (v7.98 ki maksimizira cash conversion) — ta daje ANNUALIZED RETURN VIEW z returnGrade in riskAdjustedReturn. Razlika od profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira compounding reinvest rate) — ta maksimizira ANNUALIZED RETURN na HELD inventory z returnVsBenchmark in excessReturn. "Current: 52% annualized (8 items, 10000€ deployed, 30d hold). Benchmark 10% → excess +42pp. Maximized: 95% (uplift +43pp, vs benchmark +85pp, grade A). Risk-adjusted: 78%. Optimal hold: 14d." current { totalCapitalDeployed € [0, 500000], heldInventoryCount, avgHoldDays [1, 365], portfolioAnnualizedReturn % [-100, 1000] (weighted by capital), benchmarkReturn % [0, 100] (default 10% = S&P 500), excessReturn pp [-200, 1000] (= portfolio − benchmark), avgUnrealizedProfit € [-100000, 100000] }. perItem Array<{ tradeId, title, category, capitalDeployed €, estValue €, unrealizedProfit €, holdDays [1, 365], annualizedReturn % [-100, 1000] (= (unrealizedProfit/capital) × (365/holdDays) × 100), currentReturn % [-100, 1000] (non-annualized), aiRisk [0, 100] (from Listing.aiRisk) }>. maximization { maximizedAnnualizedReturn % [-100, 1000] (≥ current, ≤ current × 1.8 ali +300pp), returnUplift pp [0, 500], returnMaximizationLevers 4 { lever FASTER_TURNOVER/HIGHER_MARGIN/BETTER_SOURCING/LOWER_FEES, potentialGain pp [0, 500], action max 200 (slovenski) }, returnVsBenchmark pp [-200, 1000] (= maximized − benchmark), optimalHoldTime dni [1, 365] (hold time ki maksimizira annualized return), returnProjection 3 { months 3/6/12, projectedReturn % [-100, 1000] (= maximized × months/12), projectedProfit € [-100000, 1000000] (= totalCapital × projectedReturn/100) }, returnGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (A+ če maximized ≥ 5x benchmark (≥50%) ali uplift ≥ 100pp, A ≥ 3x/60, B ≥ 2x/30, C ≥ 1.5x/15, D ≥ 1x/5, else F), riskAdjustedReturn % [-100, 1000] (= maximized − (avgAiRisk/100) × 0.30 × maximized) }. Anti-hallucination: returns [-100, 1000], hold time [1, 365]. 6h cache (key `inventory-annualized-return-maximizer:${heldItemIdsHash}` — invalidira ko held inventory se spremeni). Deterministic fallback (per-item annualized return + portfolio weighted avg + 4 levers z potentialGain + optimal hold time iz sold12m + 3/6/12 month projection + benchmark comparison + risk-adjusted return + grade). Empty-state fallback če 0 HELD (z estValue) in SOLD trades → grade F.

**v8.05 — AI Profit Acceleration Maximizer & AI Deal Source Capital Efficiency Maximizer & AI Inventory Turnover Yield Maximizer (3 funkcije — GROWTH RATE ACCELERATION & CAPITAL EFFICIENCY MAXIMIZATION & TURNOVER YIELD MAXIMIZATION focus):**
- **AI Profit Acceleration Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA GROWTH RATE profita (ne samo profit ampak kako hitro raste). "Tvoj profit raste 8% na mesec. Z 5 acceleration akcijami bi lahko rasel 15% na mesec — do 10000€ v 8 mesecih namesto 14." Razlika od profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira COMPOUNDING reinvest rate) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA GROWTH RATE (1st + 2nd derivative of monthly profit). Razlika od capital-growth-maximizer (v7.99 ki maksimizira compounding capital growth) — ta maksimizira ACCELERATION (2nd derivat) z accelerationLevers in maximizedGrowthRate. Razlika od profit-scale-engine (v8.02 ki scale-a cel business z phased plan) — ta maksimizira RATE OF GROWTH (pospešek), ne scale. Razlika od profit-multiplier-engine (v8.00 ki multiplicira profit z 8 levers) — ta fokusira na GROWTH ACCELERATION z accelerationScenarios (CONSERVATIVE/BALANCED/AGGRESSIVE). Razlika od revenue-growth-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira revenue growth) — ta maksimizira PROFIT GROWTH RATE (ne revenue), z growthAcceleration (2nd deriv) in growthVelocity. Razlika od profit-trajectory-forecaster (v7.72 ki FORECAST-a trajectory) — ta MAXIMIZIRA acceleration. Razlika od profit-growth-predictor (v7.81 ki PREDICT-a growth stage) — ta MAXIMIZIRA growth rate z actions. Razlika od profit-horizon-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira profit per horizon) — ta maksimizira RATE OF GROWTH z 3 scenarios in timeTo10kProfit. Razlika od profit-momentum-tracker (ki track-a momentum) — ta daje ACCELERATION LEVERS z maximizedGrowthRate. "Current: 1200€/mo profit, growth 8%/mo (1st deriv), acceleration +0.4%/mo² (2nd deriv), velocity +96€/mo. Scenarios: CONSERVATIVE → 11% growth (10000€ v 14m), BALANCED → 15% growth (10000€ v 8m), AGGRESSIVE → 22% growth (10000€ v 5m). Maximized growth: 15%/mo (BALANCED — sustainable). Acceleration levers: Sourcing (+3%/mo), Volume (+2%/mo), Pricing (+1.5%/mo), Hold time (-1d = +1%/mo), Categories (+1%/mo), Quality (+0.5%/mo). Projected: 6m 2850€/mo, 12m 6800€/mo. Time to 10k profit: 8 months. Grade: B. Risks: AGGRESSIVE → burnout (75% probability), CONSERVATIVE → slower than optimal." current { currentMonthlyProfit € [0, 100000] (avg last 3 months), currentGrowthRate %/mo [-50, 200] (1st deriv = CAGR monthly), growthAcceleration %/mo² [-50, 200] (2nd deriv = difference of slopes), growthVelocity €/mo [-50000, 200000] (linear regression slope), monthsAnalyzed, latestMonthProfit, firstMonthProfit }. maximization { accelerationScenarios 3 elementi { scenario CONSERVATIVE/BALANCED/AGGRESSIVE, projectedGrowthRate %/mo [-50, 200] (CONSERVATIVE ≈ 1.4× current, BALANCED ≈ 1.9×, AGGRESSIVE ≈ 2.8×), timeToTarget mesecev [1, 120] (koliko da doseže 10000€/mo profit), requirements 2-4 stringi (slovenski), projectedProfit6m € [0, 200000], projectedProfit12m € [0, 200000], finalMonthlyProfit € [0, 100000] (starting × (1+g)^12) }, accelerationLevers 5-7 { lever, currentState, potential, growthUplift %/mo [0, 20], action }, maximizedGrowthRate %/mo [-50, 200] (tipično BALANCED), growthAccelerationActions 4-7 stringov (slovenski), projectedProfit6m/12m, timeTo10kProfit mesecev [1, 120], accelerationGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (A+ če maximized ≥ 25%/mo ali uplift ≥ 10pp, A ≥ 18/8, B ≥ 12/5, C ≥ 8/3, D ≥ 4/1, else F), accelerationRisks 3-5 stringov (burnout, capital injection, market saturation, focus dilution) }. Anti-hallucination: growth rates [-50, 200], profits [0, 200000]. 6h cache (key `profit-acceleration-maximizer:${currentMonth}`). Deterministic fallback (monthly series CAGR + linear slope + 2nd deriv + 3 scenarios z multiplier + 6 levers). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trades → grade F.
- **AI Deal Source Capital Efficiency Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA CAPITAL EFFICIENCY per source — kateri source-i uporabljajo kapital najbolj učinkovito (profit per euro deployed per day). "Bolha daje 0.85€ profit per euro per day, Vinted 0.42€ — prestavi 500€ iz Vinted v Bolha za +35% efficiency." Razlika od deal-source-profit-per-trade-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira profit per trade €) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA CAPITAL EFFICIENCY (profit per euro per day). Razlika od deal-source-margin-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira margin %) — ta maksimizira CAPITAL EFFICIENCY z capitalReallocation. Razlika od deal-source-roi-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira ROI per source) — ta maksimizira profit-per-euro-per-day (časovno-tehtan ROI). Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per HELD item) — ta maksimizira CAPITAL EFFICIENCY per SOURCE (zgodovinski sold). Razlika od inventory-cash-yield-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira cash yield čez portfolio) — ta maksimizira per-source capital efficiency z capitalReallocation plan-om. Razlika od inventory-profit-per-day-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira daily profit per item) — ta maksimizira DAILY PROFIT PER EURO per source. Razlika od deal-source-profit-maximizer (v7.97 ki maksimizira total profit per source) — ta maksimizira EFFICIENCY (profit/capital/day), ne absolutni profit. Razlika od deal-source-volume-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira VOLUME per source) — ta maksimizira CAPITAL EFFICIENCY (kvaliteta kapitala, ne kvantiteta trade-ov). Razlika od profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira compounding reinvest rate) — ta daje per-source capital efficiency z efficiencyMaximizationAction in capitalReallocation. "Bolha: 4500€ invested, 3200€ profit, 22d avg hold → 0.85€/€/d efficiency (score 51). Vinted: 1800€ invested, 280€ profit, 14d → 0.42€/€/d (score 25). Avtonet: 6200€ invested, 5400€ profit, 18d → 1.21€/€/d (score 73). Maximization: Bolha INCREASE_CAPITAL (+800€), Vinted REDUCE_CAPITAL (-500€), Avtonet INCREASE_CAPITAL (+1200€). Portfolio: 0.65€/€/d → 0.78€/€/d (+0.13 uplift, +20% efficiency)." Per source metrics { totalInvested € [0, 500000], totalProfit € [-10000, 100000], avgHoldDays [1, 365], tradeCount, profitPerEuroDeployed [-1, 10] (total profit / total invested), profitPerEuroPerDay [0, 10] (profitPerEuro / avgHoldDays), capitalEfficiencyScore [0, 100] }. maximization per source { efficiencyMaximizationAction INCREASE_CAPITAL/REDUCE_HOLD_TIME/IMPROVE_PROFIT_MARGIN/DIVERSIFY_WITHIN/REDUCE_CAPITAL, projectedEfficiency €/€/d [0, 10] (≥ current, ≤ × 1.5 ali +2.0), efficiencyUplift €/€/d [0, 5], capitalReallocation € [-50000, 50000] (positive = shift TO, negative = shift FROM) }. portfolio { currentCapitalEfficiency (weighted avg by invested), maximizedCapitalEfficiency, totalEfficiencyUplift pp, capitalReallocationPlan Array<{ fromSource, toSource, amount €, rationale, projectedEfficiencyGain }>, totalCapital }. Anti-hallucination: scores [0, 100], efficiency [0, 10]. 6h cache (key `deal-source-capital-efficiency-maximizer:${currentMonth}`). Deterministic fallback (per-source efficiency hevristika z 5 actions, portfolio reallocation plan-from-low-to-high efficiency). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trades.
- **AI Inventory Turnover Yield Maximizer** — AI najde OPTIMALNI TURNOVER RATE ki maksimizira ANNUALIZED YIELD. Prehitro = nizka margin per trade. Prepočasi = kapital vezan. "Tvoj optimalni turnover je 3.2x/mesec kar da 85% annual yield — hitreje od trenutnega 2.5x in bolj profitabilno." Razlika od inventory-yield-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira yield % per item z yieldGrade) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA TURNOVER RATE za max ANNUALIZED YIELD z yieldCurve (7 točk: 1x-7x/month). Razlika od inventory-cash-yield-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira annualized cash yield čez portfolio z benchmark) — ta maksimizira TURNOVER RATE (frequency of trades) z optimalTurnoverRate in breakEvenTurnover. Razlika od inventory-turnover-accelerator-pro (v7.85 ki accelerira turnover per item z PRICE_DROP/RELIST/CROSS_POST) — ta MAXIMIZIRA YIELD pri optimalnem turnover rate (curve optimization, ne per-item action). Razlika od inventory-turnover-optimizer (v7.50 ki optimizira turnover) — ta maksimizira ANNUALIZED YIELD z yieldCurve. Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per item z reallocation) — ta maksimizira YIELD čez TURNOVER rate z optimalInventorySize. Razlika od inventory-roi-maximizer-pro (v7.99 ki maksimizira ROI per item) — ta maksimizira ANNUALIZED YIELD (ROI × turnover). Razlika od inventory-profit-per-day-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira daily profit per item) — ta maksimizira YIELD (annualized ROI) z turnover optimization. Razlika od inventory-turnover-profit-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira profit per turnover cycle) — ta maksimizira YIELD čez turnover rate (1x-7x curve). Razlika od inventory-cash-conversion-maximizer (v7.98 ki maksimizira cash conversion) — ta daje YIELD CURVE z breakEvenTurnover in optimalInventorySize. Razlika od profit-velocity-maximizer (v7.98 ki maksimizira €/day velocity) — ta maksimizira annualized YIELD (ROI × turnover) z yieldCurve 7 točk. "Current: 2.5x/mo turnover, 30% avg ROI, 60% annual yield. Yield curve: 1x → 360% (premium, slow), 3x → 850% (optimal), 7x → 240% (margin squeeze). Optimal turnover: 3.2x/mo → 85% annual yield (+25pp uplift). Actions: Povečaj turnover z avtomatiziranim listing refresh, ciljaj 9-dnevni cycle time, optimal inventory 18 item-ov. Grade: B. Break-even turnover: 0.5x/mo." current { currentTurnoverRate x/month [0, 20] (= (30/avgHoldDays) × scaling factor), currentYield % [-100, 1000] (= avgROI × turnover × 12), currentMonthlyProfit €, avgROIPerTrade % [-100, 500], avgHoldDays [1, 365], heldInventoryCount, heldCapitalDeployed € }. maximization { yieldCurve 7 točk { turnoverRate x/month [0, 20] (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7), projectedYield % [-100, 1000] (= (ROI × turnover × 12), ROI drops 8% per unit above base), projectedMonthlyProfit € [0, 500000], projectedAnnualProfit € [0, 500000], isOptimal boolean, description (max 200, slovenski) }, optimalTurnoverRate x/month [0, 20] (z max projectedYield — tipično 2-4x), maximizedYield % [-100, 1000], yieldUplift pp [-200, 500] (maximized − current), turnoverYieldActions 4-7 stringov (slovenski), optimalInventorySize [0, 1000] (ideal # items za optimal turnover), turnoverYieldGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (A+ če maximized ≥ 200% ali uplift ≥ 50pp, A ≥ 100/30, B ≥ 50/15, C ≥ 25/5, D > 0, else F), breakEvenTurnover x/month [0, 20] (minimum za positive annual yield) }. Anti-hallucination: rates [0, 20], yields [-100, 1000]. 6h cache (key `inventory-turnover-yield-maximizer:${currentMonth}`). **v8.04 — AI Profit Compounding Maximizer & AI Deal Source Profit Per Trade Maximizer & AI Inventory Cash Yield Maximizer (3 funkcije — COMPOUNDING MAXIMIZATION & PER-TRADE PROFIT MAXIMIZATION & CASH YIELD MAXIMIZATION focus):**
- **AI Profit Compounding Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING EFFECT reinvestiranega profita za EXPONENTIAL growth. Prikazuje kako reinvestiranje X% profita pri Y% ROI produces exponential growth vs linear. "Če reinvestiraš 80% profita pri 25% ROI per cycle, tvojih 1000€ postane 9536€ v 12 ciklih — vs 4000€ z linear growth." Razlika od capital-growth-maximizer (v7.99 ki maksimizira compounding capital growth z growth rate) — ta MAXIMIZIRA COMPOUNDING EFFECT z reinvestRate scenarios (50%/60%/70%/80%/90%/100%) in optimalReinvestRate. Razlika od profit-scale-engine (v8.02 ki SCALE-A cel business z phased plan) — ta maksimizira COMPOUNDING z reinvestRate optimization. Razlika od profit-horizon-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira profit per horizon) — ta maksimizira COMPOUNDING z linearVsCompounding comparison in breakEvenTime. Razlika od profit-multiplier-engine (v8.00 ki multiplicira profit z 8 levers) — ta fokusira na COMPOUNDING reinvest rate optimization z month-by-month 24m projection. Razlika od revenue-growth-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira REVENUE growth) — ta maksimizira COMPOUNDING PROFIT (bottom-line) z exponential growth projection. Razlika od profit-velocity-maximizer (v7.98 ki maksimizira €/day velocity) — ta daje COMPOUNDING view z compoundingMultiplier in breakEvenTime. Razlika od inventory-yield-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira yield per item) — ta maksimizira COMPOUNDING čez cel portfolio z reinvest scenarios. "Current: 1500€/mo profit, 20% reinvest rate, 35% avg ROI per cycle, 30d cycle time. Scenarios: 50% reinvest → 9400€ v 12m, 80% reinvest → 28500€ v 12m, 100% reinvest → 56200€ v 12m. Optimal reinvest rate: 80% (max capital growth while maintaining 300€/mo cash flow). Compounding projection 24m: M1 1500, M2 1800, M3 2160, ..., M24 12400. Linear vs Compounding: 18000€ (linear) vs 82500€ (compounding at 80% reinvest) — 4.6x advantage. Compounding grade: B. Break-even: compounding overtakes linear v M3." current { currentMonthlyProfit € [0, 1000000], currentReinvestRate % [0, 100], currentCycleTime dni [1, 365], avgROI % [-50, 500], startingCapital € [0, 1000000] }. maximization { compoundingScenarios 6 Array<{ reinvestRate % [50, 100] (50/60/70/80/90/100), monthlyGrowthRate % [0, 200], projectedCapital12m € [0, 1000000], projectedProfit12m € [0, 1000000], compoundingMultiplier x [1.0, 100.0] }>, optimalReinvestRate % [50, 100], maximizedCompoundingProjection 24 Array<{ month 1-24, capitalStart € [0, 1000000], profit € [0, 1000000], reinvested € [0, 1000000], cashFlow € [0, 1000000], capitalEnd € [0, 1000000] }>, linearVsCompounding { linear12mProfit € [0, 1000000], compounding12mProfit € [0, 1000000], advantageMultiple x [1.0, 100.0], breakEvenMonth [1, 24] }, compoundingAccelerationActions 4-8 stringov (max 200, slovenski), compoundingGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (A+ če multiplier ≥ 8x ali advantage ≥ 5x, A ≥ 5/3, B ≥ 3/2, C ≥ 2/1.5, D ≥ 1.5/1.2, else F), breakEvenTime month [1, 24] (crossover point) }. Anti-hallucination: rates [0, 100], multipliers [1.0, 100.0], capital [0, 1000000]. 6h cache (key `profit-compounding-maximizer:${currentMonth}`). Deterministic fallback (6 reinvest scenarios, optimal reinvest rate, 24m projection, linear-vs-compounding z break-even, 8 acceleration actions, grade). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trades → grade F.
- **AI Deal Source Profit Per Trade Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE za vsak source — kateri source-i dajejo najvišji profit PER INDIVIDUAL DEAL in kako povečati. Razlika od deal-source-margin-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira margin % per source) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA PROFIT PER TRADE € (absolutni profit, ne %), z profitPerTradeAction (TARGET_HIGHER_VALUE/NEGOTIATE_BETTER/REDUCE_FEES/IMPROVE_QUALITY/SHIFT_TO_PREMIUM) in bestTradeProfile. Razlika od deal-source-volume-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira VOLUME per source) — ta maksimizira PROFIT PER TRADE (kvaliteta, ne kvantiteta). Razlika od deal-profit-margin-enhancer-pro (v8.01 ki enhanca margin per HELD item) — ta maksimizira profit per trade PER SOURCE (zgodovinski sold). - **AI Inventory Cash Yield Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA CASH YIELD — annualizirani return rate na kapitalu deployed v inventory. Kot financial investor ki optimizira portfolio yield. Razlika od inventory-yield-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira yield % per item z yieldGrade) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA CASH YIELD (annualized return rate na capital deployed) čez PORTFOLIO z currentCashYield/maximizedCashYield in benchmarkYield. Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per item z reallocation) — ta maksimizira CASH YIELD % z annualized view in yieldComparisonTable. Razlika od inventory-roi-maximizer-pro (v7.99 ki maksimizira ROI per item) — ta maksimizira CASH YIELD z optimalHoldTime in benchmarkYield. Razlika od deal-profit-margin-enhancer-pro (v8.01 ki enhanca margin per item) — ta maksimizira CASH YIELD (ne margin) z yieldVsBenchmark. Razlika od inventory-profit-per-day-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira daily profit) — ta daje PER-ITEM annualized yield analizo z yieldComparisonTable. Razlika od profit-horizon-maximizer (v8.03 ki maksimizira profit per horizon) — ta fokusira na PORTFOLIO CASH YIELD z benchmark comparison. Razlika od profit-compounding-maximizer (v8.04 ki maksimizira compounding reinvest) — ta maksimizira CASH YIELD (annualized yield %, ne reinvest compounding). Razlika od profit-multiplier-engine (v8.00 ki multiplicira profit z 8 levers) — ta fokusira na CASH YIELD % z yieldGrade in yieldVsBenchmark. "Current: 8500€ deployed, portfolio cash yield 45% annualized (benchmark 32%), 13% above benchmark. Maximized: 78% annualized (+33pp uplift). Item analysis: iPhone 13 yield 421% annualized (hold 25d), PS5 yield 48% (hold 60d), TV yield -28% (hold 90d). Maximization: HOLD iPhone (already excellent), SELL PS5 (slow yield), REPRICE TV (negative yield). Yield comparison: 8 items, 5 above benchmark, 3 below. Optimal hold time: 30 dni (sweet spot za max annualized yield). Yield grade: B (above benchmark, room for optimization). **v8.03 — AI Profit Horizon Maximizer & AI Deal Source Margin Maximizer & AI Inventory Yield Maximizer (3 funkcije — HORIZON MAXIMIZATION & MARGIN MAXIMIZATION & YIELD MAXIMIZATION focus):**
- **AI Profit Horizon Maximizer** — AI identificira MAXIMUM profit achievable nad različnimi časovnimi horizonti (7/30/90/365 dni) in kaj je potrebnega za dosego vsakega. "V 7 dneh lahko narediš 500€, v 30 dneh 2000€, v 90 dneh 8000€ — tukaj je kaj vsak zahteva." Razlika od profit-scale-engine (v8.02 ki SCALE-A cel business z 12-mesečnim phased plan) — ta MAXIMIZIRA PROFIT PER HORIZON z bestHorizon (profit/time ratio) in acceleration actions (kako doseči longer horizons FASTER). Razlika od revenue-growth-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira REVENUE growth) — ta maksimizira PROFIT (bottom-line) per horizon z requirements matrix. Razlika od inventory-profit-per-day-maximizer (v8.02 ki maksimizira daily profit) — ta daje MULTI-HORIZON view (7d/30d/90d/365d) z feasibility (EASY/MODERATE/HARD/AMBITIOUS). Razlika od profit-multiplier-engine (v8.00 ki multiplicira profit z 8 levers) — ta fokusira na HORIZON MAXIMIZATION z feasibility in bottlenecks per horizon. Razlika od capital-growth-maximizer (v7.99 ki maksimizira compounding capital growth) — ta daje HORIZON-BASED profit plan z cumulative 12-month projection. Razlika od profit-velocity-maximizer (v7.98 ki maksimizira €/day velocity) — ta daje 4 HORIZONS z requirements in bottlenecks per horizon. "Current: 65€/dan profit rate, 8500€ deployed capital, 18 items inventory. Horizons: 7d → 500€ max (EASY, requires: inventory 18, capital 8500€, 3 trades, ROI 35%). 30d → 2000€ max (MODERATE, requires: inventory 25, capital 11000€, 12 trades, ROI 38%). 90d → 8000€ max (HARD, requires: inventory 35, capital 18000€, 40 trades, ROI 42%). 365d → 35000€ max (AMBITIOUS, requires: inventory 60, capital 35000€, 180 trades, ROI 45%). Best horizon: 90d (8000€ / 90d = 88.9€/dan ratio — best profit/time). Acceleration: 90d achievable in 60d z automation + VA. Bottlenecks: 7d (limited inventory), 30d (capital gap), 90d (sourcing capacity), 365d (market depth). Cumulative 12m: M1 800, M2 950, ..., M12 4500." current { currentDailyProfitRate € [0, 200000/365], currentMonthlyProfit € [0, 200000], currentInventoryValue € [0, 1000000], currentCapitalDeployed € [0, 1000000], avgROI % [-50, 500], avgHoldDays dni [0, 730] }. maximization { horizons 4 Array<{ period 7d/30d/90d/365d, maxAchievableProfit € [0, 200000], requirements { inventoryNeeded [0, 10000], capitalNeeded € [0, 1000000], tradesNeeded [0, 5000], avgROI % [-50, 500] }, actions 3-6 stringov (max 200, slovenski), feasibility EASY/MODERATE/HARD/AMBITIOUS, confidenceLevel 0-100 }>, bestHorizon 7d/30d/90d/365d, profitAccelerationActions 4-8 stringov (max 200, slovenski), horizonBottlenecks 4 [{ period, bottleneck max 200, mitigation max 200 }], cumulativeProjection 12 [{ month 1-12, projectedProfit €, cumulativeProfit € }] }. Deterministic fallback (4 horizons z feasibility matrix, 8 acceleration actions, 4 bottlenecks, 12-month projection). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trades.
- **AI Deal Source Margin Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA PROFIT MARGINS PER SOURCE — kateri source-i imajo najboljše margins in kako izboljšati margins iz vsakega. Razlika od deal-source-roi-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira ROI per source) — ta MAKSIMIZIRA MARGIN % per source (ne ROI %), z marginMaximizationAction (IMPROVE_PRICING/REDUCE_COSTS/OPTIMIZE_FEES/SHIFT_CATEGORY_MIX/EXIT) in marginMaximizationLevers. Razlika od deal-source-profit-maximizer (v7.97 ki maksimizira total profit per source) — ta maksimizira MARGIN % z levers, ne total profit €. Razlika od profit-scale-engine (v8.02 ki scale-a cel business) — ta daje PER-SOURCE margin maximization z marginUplift in sourceMarginRanking. Razlika od revenue-growth-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira revenue growth) — ta maksimizira MARGIN % (ne revenue), z margin uplift per source. - **AI Inventory Yield Maximizer** — AI MAXIMIZIRA YIELD (profit as % of capital deployed) na HELD inventory. **v8.02 — AI Profit Scale Engine & AI Deal Source Volume Maximizer & AI Inventory Profit Per Day Maximizer (3 funkcije — BUSINESS SCALING & VOLUME MAXIMIZATION & DAILY PROFIT MAXIMIZATION focus):**
- **AI Profit Scale Engine** — AI identificira kako SCALE-ATI profit za EXPONENTIAL growth — NE optimizira trenutne operacije, ampak načrtuje scaling CELEGA business-a. "You're making 2000€/month. To scale to 10,000€/month you need: 3x more inventory, 2x faster turnover, and 1.5x better margins." Razlika od profit-multiplier-engine (v8.00 ki MULTIPLICIRA profit z 8 levers) — ta SCALE-A cel business z exponential growth plan (phased: 2x → 3x → 5x), ne compounding multiplier. Razlika od revenue-growth-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira REVENUE growth) — ta SCALE-A PROFIT (bottom-line), ne revenue (top-line), in dodaja scaling bottlenecks + capacity analysis. Razlika od inventory-capital-efficiency-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira capital efficiency per item) — ta daje GLOBAL scale plan z bottlenecks in phased timeline (ne per-item). Razlika od capital-growth-maximizer (v7.99 ki maksimizira compounding capital growth) — ta maksimizira SCALE FACTOR (multiplier na monthly profit z bottlenecks). Razlika od profit-velocity-maximizer (v7.98 ki maksimizira €/day velocity) — ta daje PHASED scale plan (2x/3x/5x) z bottleneck analizo. Razlika od inventory-growth-planner (ki planira inventory growth) — ta SCALE-A profit preko 3 axes (inventory × turnover × margin) z capacity assessment. "Current: 2000€/mo profit, 18 items inventory, 12 trades/mo, avgProfitPerTrade 167€, ROI 35%. Scale capacity: capital 5000€ available, time 20h/wk, logistics 8 items/mo. Bottlenecks: capital (15k gap), sourcing (need 3x more deals), time (need 40h/wk). Action plan: Phase 1 (3mo) 2x=4000€, Phase 2 (6mo) 3x=6000€, Phase 3 (12mo) 5x=10000€. Timeline: 12 months to target. Risk: capital gap, market depth, sourcing burnout. Deterministic fallback (3-phase plan, 5 bottlenecks, 4 risks). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trades → grade F.
- **AI Deal Source Volume Maximizer** — AI maksimizira trade VOLUME per source — kako dobiti VEČ deal-ov iz vsakega source-a BREZ dilutiranja quality. Fokus na scaling VOLUME (več trades) namesto ROI ali margin. "Bolha je trenutno 8 trades/mo, lahko 18 trades/mo z broader categories + cross-posting." Razlika od deal-source-roi-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira ROI per source) — ta MAXIMIZIRA VOLUME per source (več trades), ne ROI %. Razlika od deal-source-profit-maximizer (v7.97 ki maksimizira profit per source) — ta maksimizira VOLUME (število trades) z quality maintenance strategy, ne profit €. Razlika od revenue-growth-maximizer (v8.01 ki maksimizira top-line revenue) — ta daje PER-SOURCE volume maximization z volume levers + projected 30d volume. Razlika od deal-source-momentum-analyzer (ki analizira momentum) — ta MAXIMIZIRA volume z actionable levers (more search terms, broader categories, new monitors, cross-posting). Razlika od deal-source-trend-analyzer (ki analizira trende) — ta daje PROJECTED 30d volume + 3/6/12m forecast + portfolio-level volume uplift. Vinted SCALE_GRADUALLY → 6 trades/mo (+2 uplift). Quality strategy: dealScore ≥60 threshold, avgProfit ≥80% trenutnega. Capital requirement: 5000€. Time requirement: 15h/wk. **v8.01 — AI Revenue Growth Maximizer & AI Deal Profit Margin Enhancer Pro & AI Inventory Capital Efficiency Maximizer (3 funkcije — REVENUE GROWTH & MARGIN ENHANCEMENT & CAPITAL EFFICIENCY focus):**
- **AI Revenue Growth Maximizer** — AI maksimizira REVENUE GROWTH (top-line revenue, NE samo profit). "Your revenue is 5000€/mo but could be 12,000€/mo with 3 expansion actions." Razlika od profit-multiplier-engine (v8.00 ki MULTIPLICIRA profit z 8 levers) — ta MAXIMIZIRA REVENUE (top-line sales), ne profit. Razlika od deal-source-roi-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira ROI per source) — ta fokusira na REVENUE GROWTH rate in category expansion (ne ROI %). Razlika od inventory-turnover-profit-maximizer (v8.00 ki maksimizira turnover-profit balance) — ta maksimizira TOTAL REVENUE preko volume scaling in category expansion. Razlika od capital-growth-maximizer (v7.99 ki maksimizira capital growth) — ta maksimizira REVENUE (top-line), ne capital. Razlika od revenue-stream-optimizer (v7.94 ki optimira revenue streams) — ta MAXIMIZIRA revenue z growth actions in category expansion + 3m/6m/12m projection + timeToDoubleRevenue. Category expansion: electronics (+2800€/mo), automotive (+1500€/mo). Volume scaling: 12→24 trades/mo (2x). Diversification: Bolha 70% → Bolha 40% + Vinted 30% + mobile.de 30%. Grade A. Time to double revenue: 87 days." current { currentMonthlyRevenue € [0, 100000], revenueGrowthRate % [0, 200], avgRevenuePerTrade € [0, 100000], tradeVolumePerMonth [0, 1000], revenueStreams Array<{ source, percentage % [0, 100], revenue € }> }. maximization { maximizedMonthlyRevenue € [0, 100000], revenueGrowthMultiplier [1.0, 5.0] (anti-hallucination), growthActions 4-6 [{ action max 200 (slovenski), expectedRevenueGain € [0, 50000], priority HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW, difficulty EASY/MEDIUM/HARD }], revenueProjection3m/6m/12m € [0, 100000], categoryExpansionOpportunities 3-5 [{ category max 50, potentialRevenue € [0, 50000], reasoning max 200 (slovenski) }], volumeScalingPlan max 400 (slovenski), revenueDiversificationStrategy max 400 (slovenski), revenueGrowthGrade A+/A/B/C/D/F (≥3.5 A+, ≥2.5 A, ≥1.8 B, ≥1.4 C, ≥1.15 D, else F), timeToDoubleRevenue dni [1, 3650] (= ln(2)/ln(1+dailyGrowth) ) }. AI-enhanced z grounding prompt (soldCount + revenue + sources + current + deterministicMaximization + caps) + anti-hallucination (revenueGrowthMultiplier CLAMPED [1.0, 5.0], gain [0, 50000], days [1, 3650], enums validirana HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW in EASY/MEDIUM/HARD in A+/A/B/C/D/F, string length limits — action max 200, reasoning max 200, plan max 400, summary max 400) + 6h cache (key `revenue-growth-maximizer:${currentMonth}` — YYYY-MM, invalidira monthly) + deterministic fallback (4 growth actions hevristika, multiplier iz gain ratio, grade iz multiplier, projections z compounding). Empty-state fallback če 0 SOLD trades → "Ni SOLD trgovin v zadnjih 12 mesecih" z aiUsed=false + empty current z 0 + empty maximization z grade F.
- **AI Deal Profit Margin Enhancer Pro** — AI ENHANCE-A profit margin na vsakem HELD item-u preko specifičnih FIZIČNIH/LISTING enhancement akcij (refurbishment, repositioning, premium packaging, better photos, improved descriptions, certification). Mitigation: sell 5 items >60d → risk -15." current { concentrationRisk (Herfindahl HHI 0-100), agingRisk (% items held >60d), marketRisk (iz recent SOLD profit trend + volatility), liquidityRisk (avg hold days + no-contact rate), categoryRisk (avg aiRisk 1-10 × 10), overallRiskScore 0-100 (25% concentration + 25% aging + 20% market + 15% liquidity + 15% category), currentRiskLevel LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL }. forecast { projectedRisk30d/60d/90d (clamped [0, 100], ±15 od deterministic z daily composite change iz aging growth + concentration growth + market reversion + liquidity growth), riskTrend IMPROVING/STABLE/WORSENING (iz 90d delta ±4), projectedRiskLevel LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/CRITICAL, confidenceLevel 0-100 (base 55 + concentration/aging stickiness ±10/8 - market volatility 12 + risk visibility). analysis { emergingRiskFactors 2-5 z risk max 200/probability 0-100/impact max 150/timeline max 30 (iz kateri komponente rastejo), riskHotspots 2-5 z item/category/riskScore 0-100/reason max 150 (top 5 highest-risk items z daysHeld + aiRisk + contact + dealScore), riskMitigationPlan 2-5 z action max 200/priority HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW/riskReduction 0-100/timeline max 30 (iz highest risk komponent), riskTolerance { level CONSERVATIVE/BALANCED/AGGRESSIVE (iz portfolio size), assessment max 400, acceptable boolean (risk < 60) } }. AI-enhanced z grounding (current + deterministicForecast + deterministicAnalysis + portfolio summary + caps) + anti-hallucination (projectedRisk30d/60d/90d ±15 od deterministic in clamped [0, 100], confidenceLevel ±15 in clamped [0, 100]; riskTrend/projectedRiskLevel/riskTolerance.level/priority validirana proti enum; emergingRiskFactors risk max 200/impact max 150/timeline max 30, riskHotspots item max 80/category max 40/reason max 150, riskMitigationPlan action max 200/timeline max 30, riskTolerance.assessment max 400; summary max 400) + 6h cache (key `portfolio-risk-forecaster:${JSON.stringify(heldItemIds)}`) + deterministic fallback (compute iz current risk × daily change). - **Market Sentiment Trend Analyzer** — pure DB analizira kako SENTIMENT trga spreminja čez čas — track-a sentiment score trends čez 26 tednov, identificira sentiment cikle in detektira sentiment turning points. Trend: +2.1/wk (IMPROVING). Last trough: W12. Next peak: ~W22." current { sentimentScore 0-100, classification VERY_HOT/HOT/WARM/COOL/COLD, currentSentimentPhase RECOVERY/EXPANSION/PEAK/CONTRACTION/TROUGH (iz current score + 26w trend + acceleration + percentile rank) }. trends { sentimentTrend26w (linear regression slope per week), sentimentTrend3m (last 13 weeks slope), sentimentDirection IMPROVING/STABLE/DECLINING (iz ±0.5 threshold), sentimentVolatility (stddev weekly scores), sentimentMomentum (acceleration = slope second half - slope first half) }. weeklyData [{ week ISO date, sentimentScore, classification, listingVelocity, sellThroughRate, prilikaRate }] (26 tednov z istimi 5 signalnimi utežmi kot market-sentiment-pulse: 20% velocity + 20% priceTrend + 15% dealQuality + 25% sellThrough + 20% prilika). turningPoints { sentimentPeaks [{ week, score }], sentimentTroughs [{ week, score }], lastTurningPoint { week, direction UP/DOWN, score } | null }. cycleAnalysis { avgSentimentCycleLength (avg weeks between peaks/troughs), sentimentCyclePosition (EARLY_RECOVERY/MID_EXPANSION/LATE_EXPANSION/AT_PEAK/EARLY_CONTRACTION/AT_TROUGH), nextPredictedPeak (ISO date ali null — iz avg cycle + last peak) }. byCategory [{ category (iz monitor.source), currentSentiment, trend, direction, rank }]. insights { bestImprovingCategory, worstDecliningCategory, advice max 500 (iz phase + direction + best/worst category) }. Compute: query listings zadnjih 180 dni (price, dealScore, aiVerdict, firstSeenAt, isBookmarked, contactStatus, monitor.source), group by ISO week (26 weeks aligned to Monday), per week compute sentiment (5-signal weighted composite — ista logika kot market-sentiment-pulse vendar per-week z previous-week baseline), linear regression slopes za 26w + 3m, compute volatility in momentum (acceleration), classify phase iz score + trend + acceleration + percentile rank, detect turning points (local maxima/minima), compute avg cycle length between peaks, predict next peak iz avg cycle + last peak, per-category analysis (≥4 weeks). Avto: -5% (depreciating). 65% of items appreciating." portfolio { totalBuyPrice, totalCurrentEstValue (sum aiEstimatedValue or buyPrice fallback), totalUnrealizedGain, portfolioAppreciationPercent, avgAppreciationRate (mean monthly %), appreciatingItemCount, depreciatingItemCount, flatItemCount, appreciationRatio (%) }. perItem [{ tradeId, title, category, buyPrice, currentEstValue, unrealizedGain, unrealizedGainPercent, daysHeld, appreciationRate (monthly % = unrealizedGainPercent / daysHeld × 30), appreciationStatus APPRECIATING/FLAT/DEPRECIATING (±2% threshold) }]. byCategory [{ category, itemCount, totalBuyPrice, totalEstValue, avgAppreciationPercent, appreciationRank 1=best }]. byAgeBucket [{ ageBucket (0-7d/7-14d/14-30d/30-60d/60-90d/90d+), itemCount, avgAppreciationPercent, trend APPRECIATING_MORE/STABLE/DEPRECIATING_MORE (vs first bucket ±5%) }]. trend { recentItemsAppreciation (<30d items avg), olderItemsAppreciation (>60d items avg), appreciationTrend ACCELERATING/STABLE/DECELERATING (iz delta ±5) }. insights { bestAppreciatingCategory, worstDepreciatingCategory, collectibleCandidates (items >60d AND appreciating — top 5), liquidationCandidates (items >30d AND depreciating — top 5), advice max 500 }. Compute: query HELD trades z linked Listing (aiEstimatedValue, monitor.source), per item compute currentEstValue (aiEstimatedValue ali buyPrice fallback), unrealizedGain, unrealizedGainPercent, daysHeld, appreciationRate (monthly), appreciationStatus; portfolio aggregation (totalBuyPrice, totalCurrentEstValue, appreciationRatio); per-category analysis z avg appreciation + rank; age bucket analysis (6 buckets) z trend (APPRECIATING_MORE if older buckets appreciate more); trend (recent <30d vs older >60d items — ACCELERATING if recent appreciate more). Pure DB (NO AI). GET /api/analytics/inventory-value-appreciation-tracker. Vinted: 800€, score 58/100 (#2)." sources [{ source, displayName, components { grossProfit, revenue, cost, grossMargin (% = grossProfit/revenue × 100), markupPercent (% = (revenue-cost)/cost × 100), feeImpactPercent (% = fees/revenue × 100), volumeContribution (= trades × avgProfitPerTrade), efficiencyScore (€ per day held = profit/avgHoldDays), tradeCount, avgProfitPerTrade }, drivers { priceDriver { value (avg sell price), impact POSITIVE/NEGATIVE/NEUTRAL, detail max 200 chars } (vs market avg sell price ±5%), costDriver { value, impact, detail } (avg buy price vs avg estValue), volumeDriver { value (trade count), impact, detail } (vs market avg per source ±10%), efficiencyDriver { value (avgHoldDays), impact, detail } (vs market avg hold days ±10%) }, profitabilityScore 0-100 (30% grossMargin + 25% markupPercent + 20% volumeContribution relative + 15% efficiencyScore relative + 10% feeImpact inverse), profitabilityRank, trend { recent3mProfit, previous3mProfit, trendDirection (IMPROVING/STABLE/DECLINING iz delta ±10%), trendPercent } }]. summary { totalProfit, bestProfitSource, worstProfitSource, mostImprovedSource, avgProfitabilityScore, advice (max 500 chars) }. Compute: query SOLD trades 12m z linked Listing (za monitor.source + aiEstimatedValue), aggregate per source (grossProfit, revenue, cost, fees, tradeCount, holdDays, estValue, sellPrice, buyPrice), compute components (grossMargin, markupPercent, feeImpact, volumeContribution, efficiencyScore), compute market averages (across all sources) za driver comparisons, classify each driver impact, compute profitability score z weighted formula, rank sources, trend (recent 3m vs previous 3m profit). - **Market Cycle Detector** — identificira v kateri fazi tržnega cikla smo trenutno: ACCUMULATION (kupovalna priložnost), MARKUP (cene rastejo), DISTRIBUTION (čas za prodajo), ali DECLINE (cene padajo). Pure DB analytics — NO AI. "Market cycle: MARKUP (60% progress, 8 weeks). Prices +5%/mo, volume +10%. BUY before DISTRIBUTION phase." cycle: currentPhase (4-fazni Wyckoff-inspired cycle), cycleProgress 0-100%, cycleDuration (weeks in current phase), phaseConfidence 0-100, phaseDescription. indicators: priceTrend90d/30d (linear regression slope + UP/FLAT/DOWN direction), volumeTrend90d/30d (slope + direction), volatilityIndex (stdDev of weekly avg prices / mean × 100), dealQualityTrend (IMPROVING/STABLE/DECLINING). byCategory: per-source (Bolha/Vinted/mobile.de) phase + confidence + price/volume trend. historical: phasesLast180d (reconstructed weekly phases z weeks/startDate/endDate), mostCommonPhase. recommendation: action (BUY_AGGRESSIVELY/BUY/HOLD/SELL/SELL_AGGRESSIVELY/WAIT), reasoning, timeHorizon. Compute: query listings zadnjih 180 dni, group by ISO week, linear regression na weekly avg price + weekly volume. 4-fazna klasifikacija (ACCUMULATION = flat/low prices + low volatility, MARKUP = rising prices + rising volume, DISTRIBUTION = high/flat prices + peaking volume + high volatility, DECLINE = falling prices + declining volume). Pure DB analytics, NO AI. Razlika od market-momentum (ki da BULLISH/BEARISH/NEUTRAL score glede na trend) — ta identificira 4-fazni CYCLE (Wyckoff-inspired). Razlika od market-sentiment-pulse (ki kombinira 5 signalov v 0-100 pulse) — ta gleda CENOVNE in VOLUMSKE trende za fazno klasifikacijo. Razlika od market-saturation-forecaster (ki forecast-a saturacijo) — ta gleda 4-fazni cikel z volatilnostjo. Razlika od market-depth-analyzer (ki gleda likvidnost) — ta gleda phase-timing za buy/sell odločitve.
